# Changelog/2026/October 6
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
0.27.2
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.27.2 - The Minister Update, Greenhouse QOL, and More!
Updated: October 6, 2026
Hello! Today we're bringing you SkyBlock 0.27.2 - a massive update featuring Minister Perks, improvements to the Greenhouse, Mob Levels, and Reforge Stones, as well as some other general quality-of-life changes to the game.
## ► The Minister Update
During Mayor Aura's term, all players were able to campaign to be elected as a "Minister", to either help with or interfere with Aura's schemes. At the core of each player's election campaign was the promise that they would get a particular perk added to the game - be that a new accessory line, pet, and so on. Today, we bring you the complete set of "Minister Perks", all of which were individually designed in correspondence with each of the elected Ministers.
### ➥ Precursor Drone (thirtyvirus)
The Precursor Drone has received some changes! Now being base Legendary, players may collect pet items known as "Drone Mods" from various areas of the game and attach them to the Precursor Drone in order to grant it new base stats and perks. There are currently 3 Drone Mods but, who knows, maybe more will be added in the future:
- Drone Mod: Grungle → Forest Essence Shop, 2,500x Forest Essence
- Drone Mod: Contraband → Crafted using various Junk items sourced from the Backwater Bayou
- Drone Mod: Mining off Camera → Diamond Essence Shop, 2,500x Diamond Essence
Designer's Note: The Precursor Drone didn't release in an ideal state so we took some time to revisit it, looking to both better deliver on the customizable aspect of it and find a futureproof design that would allow for the pet to be further adjusted in the future. We saw a great suggestion on the forums (you can expect a Creative Mind in your Jerry soon!) that introduced "Drone Mods" - items that could be applied directly to the Drone to grant it base stats and perks, allowing for players to swap perks in and out depending upon their activity.
### ➥ Blood God Sigil & Potato Ring (Technodad)
Technodad's perk started a Year of the Pig event while he was in office - along with buffs to Potato Minions, and introduced two new upgrades to the Potato Talisman and Blood God Talisman. Players can craft both these upgrades - which have been gracefully bestowed upon SkyBlock by the Great Potato King himself - from items gathered from Shiny Pigs.
Designer's Note: This Minister Perk was already in the game and hasn't been adjusted as part of this release.
### ➥ Kuudra's Kidney, Lung, and Heart (Future77)
Crafted from Kuudra Tentacles, which can be purchased from paid chests after defeating Kuudra, the Kuudra's Kidney, Lung, and Heart accessory line grants some useful quality-of-life features for those looking to take on the lava kraken.
Designer's Note: This Minister Perk was already in the game and hasn't been adjusted as part of this release.
### ➥ Mining Fiesta Rework (zj2)
When Mayor Cole is elected with his Mining Fiesta perk, said Fiesta will now span his entire mayorship! Though the buffs have been toned down a bit as a result, you can purchase up to 5 Fiesta Flasks (as well as a brand-new Attribute) from Brynmor that stack additively with the base fortune buff from the fiesta, allowing you to either consume them when you're sure you have the time in order to maximize your profits, or store them up to go all-out during a future Fiesta.
Designer's Note: Fiestas were previously extremely strong but balanced out by the fact that most players could never attend all of them. However, with fiestas actually being quite important to progression, this has become very frustrating for players who struggle to attend.
As a result, we thought about the Minister's proposal and decided to weaken fiestas a bit, making them +25% Mining Fortune, but to allow them to span the entire duration of Cole's mayorship. We will see how this pans out and know that some players who attended entire Fiestas may be upset at their weakening, but the main goal here is for more players to be able to participate in fiestas and for less players to end up progression locked by them.
### ➥ Hotspot Talisman, Ring, and Artifact (Derailious)
Located in the Clay Collection (Clay being the token drop of Water Hotspot Sea Creatures), the Hotspot Talisman line grants you even more useful buffs when fishing in a Hotspot, and is crafted out of materials gathered from various Hotspot Sea Creatures and junk found in the Bayou.
Designer's Note: This Minister originally wanted a Tracking Mixin to help out new players, though this was delivered as part of the Harvest Feast update. As such, it was agreed upon that a new talisman line, which ended up being located in the Clay Collection. would be useful for early/mid-game fishers who spend a lot of their time fishing in Hotspots, regardless of whether they're treasure fishing or sea creature fishing.
### ➥ Dungeon Terminal Changes (fear5s)
Some particularly dastardly Dungeons Terminals have been made a little easier/less time-consuming thanks to the work of this generous Minister, who has dropped the amount of inputs needed to complete each of them:
- Reduced the amount of rows on the "Melody Terminal" from 4 → 3
- Reduced the amount of numbers on the "Numbers Terminal" from 14 → 10
### ➥ Beastslayer's Codex (Intrests)
The Beastslayer's Codex is a talisman given by Bramass Beastslayer upon reaching Bestiary Milestone 50. Though it starts out as a mere common accessory, the Codex upgrades as you progress through the Bestiary Milestones:
- Milestone 100:
  - Increases to UNCOMMON
  - Stats:
    - Strength: +2
    - Intelligence: +1
- Milestone 150:
  - Increases to RARE
  - Stats:
    - Strength: +3
    - Intelligence: +2
    - Crit Damage: +1
- Milestone 200:
  - Increases to EPIC
  - Stats:
    - Strength: +4
    - Intelligence: +3
    - Crit Damage: +2
    - Ability Damage: +1
- Milestone 250:
  - Increases to LEGENDARY
  - Stats:
    - Strength: +5
    - Intelligence: +4
    - Crit Damage: +3
    - Ability Damage: +2
    - Tracking: +1
- Milestone 300:
  - Increases to MYTHIC
  - Stats:
    - Strength: +6
    - Intelligence: +5
    - Crit Damage: +4
    - Ability Damage: +3
    - Tracking: +2
    - Magic Find: +1
### ➥ Eagle Pet (Kuvdra)
This Minister sought to add the Eagle Pet - a Hunting Pet exclusive to the Critter Safari. Unlocked and upgraded through the Safari Essence Shop, the Eagle Pet will accompany you whenever you enter the Safari, and has a whole host of perks to offer that'll help you out.
### ➥ Dungeon Time Saves (Celestial_Milk)
The following time saves have been added to The Catacombs, with most of them being conditional upon all players having cleared the floor at least once:
- Sped up Boss/Watcher dialogue across all floors
- Sped up cutscenes across all floors
- The Watcher now spawns its summons faster
- Sped up the following parts of F7/M7, so long as all players have 1+ completion:
  - Maxor Spawn, Crystals and Laser Reset Timings, Death, and Phase Transition
  - Storm Spawn, Death, and Phase Transition
  - Goldor Spawn, Death, and Phase Transition
  - Necron's fire rings now start at half the size of current
  - Necron Death and Phase Transition (M7)
  - Dragon Crystals now all break within 3s instead of one every 3s (M7)
  - The next Dragon now spawns immediately if all are dead (M7)
  - Dungeon now ends immediately upon completion of final phase (M7)
### ➥ Small, Medium, and Large Bait Sack (CandyPat)
The Fishing Bag has been converted into the Bait Sack! Unlocked through the Raw Cod collection, the Small Bait Sack can be upgraded all the way to the Large Bait Sack, which can hold 20,160 of each Fishing Bait found throughout the game.
The new Bait Sack line serves as a more intuitive and organized version of what was previously the Fishing Bag. It even allows you to favor a particular bait by shift-clicking it, which ensures that bait will be always be used so long as you have some of it in your sacks.
### ➥ Refractive Paint Palette (Refraction)
The Refractive Paint Palette is a new item that, like Necron's Handle and Diana's Bookshelf, can be purchased from the Regalia Room for 75m coins. It tracks unique Dyes you've obtained during your profile's lifetime and, upon completion, gives you some nice stats to boot; it truly is a must-get for any Dye hunters! The palette even starts off blank and gains a new color each time you obtain a new Dye!
Designer's Note: To keep the mostly cosmetic grind of Dyes more separated from regular gameplay we decided to make the item award Dye chance instead of Magic Find. It now serves as a nice reward for getting your first few dyes and something that'll help you on your journey towards obtaining the rarer ones.
### ➥ Powder of the People (Alaawii)
The Powder of the People accessory line has been to the game! It can be obtained and upgraded through the Dwarven Forge:
- Powder of One: Increases Mithril Powder gain by +2.5%
  - HOTM Requirement: 2
  - Material Requirement: 1x Refined Mithril, 1x Refined Titanium
  - Forging Process: 1 Hour
- Powder of The Few: Increases Mithril Powder and Gemstone Powder gain by +5%
  - HOTM Requirement: 5
  - Material Requirement: 1x Powder of One, 4x Gemstone Mixture, 32x Refined Mineral
  - Forging Process: 1 Hour
- Powder of The Many: Increases Mithril Powder, Gemstone Powder, and Glacite Powder gain by +7.5%
  - HOTM Requirement: 8
  - Material Requirement: 1x Powder of the Few, 4x Glacite Amalgamation, 32x Glossy Gemstone
  - Forging Process: 6 Hours
- Powder of the People: Increases Mithril Powder, Gemstone Powder, and Glacite Powder gain by +10%
  - Obtained via combining Powder of the People with Divan's Powder Coating in an Anvil
### ➥ Scatha-in-a-Bottle (Toadstar0)
Craft yourself a Nothing-in-a-bottle upon reaching Living Metal Heart 3! What does it do, you may ask? Nothing! However, if you take the bottle out of the Rift and into the overworld, you may feel a strange compulsion to use it to capture a Scatha. Upon doing so, you'll find yourself with a brand-new Mixin that grants a whopping +20 Tracking towards Scatha!
In addition to this new item, Worm Membranes have also been added to some Drop Tables:
- Stoneworm: 1x Worm Membrane (100%)
- Scatha: 4x Worm Membrane (100%)
### ➥ Returning Player Buffs (Endthr)
Returning players who have been offline from SkyBlock for 90 days or more will find Calvin waiting for them when they return to their Private Island. A thoughtful soul, Calvin will give returning players some handy buffs to boost their progression a little upon their return:
- A flat amount of Coins
  - +500,000-2,500,000
- Bonus Skill Wisdom
  - +5-25
- Slayer XP Boost
  - +5-25%
- Dungeons XP & Dungeons Class XP Boost
  - +5-25%
Calvin's buffs last for 7 days, so make the most of them while you have them!
### ➥ Secret Bingo #2 (arithemonkey)
This Minister desired a second Secret Bingo event, which we decided to release in April 2026 to coincide with April Fools. We were very happy with how it went - goals were discovered at a good pace, participation was high, and it seemed like players were having an all-round good time. You can expect another Secret Bingo in the future, though we don't have a concrete timeline for it just yet.
### ➥ HOTM/HOTF Loadouts (Luamon)
This Minister wanted to add Loadouts that allowed you to swap around your HOTM/HOTF trees freely instead of having to constantly reset and rebuild them. The official "Loadouts Update" was added to the game a little over a month ago as part of Hypixel SkyBlock 0.26, and contained both the desired loadouts. We were initially hesitant to include skill trees within the Loadouts system as they're not something we want players consistently swapping/resetting, but the wishes of this Minister pushed us into including them.
### ➥ Folf Shard & Dungeon Improvements (F22_Raptor)
The Folf Shard is a new RARE Attribute that increases your Speed Cap by +5-50 while in The Catacombs. It can be obtained by fusing the Soul of the Alpha Shard with any EPIC or higher shard.
In addition to this new Shard, we have also made some other changes to Dungeons as part of this perk:
- Increased the pickup range of Wither Keys and Blood Keys in the Catacombs by 5 blocks
- Increased the activation range of Fels by 5 blocks.
### ➥ Dark Auction Improvements (lejimbo)
It has been made easier to obtain the Seal of the Family accessory line:
- Reduced Dark Auction purchases required to obtain the Shady Ring from 5 → 1
- Reduced Dark Auction purchases required to obtain the Crooked Artifact from 10 → 3
- Reduced Dark Auction purchases required to obtain the Seal of the Family from 15 → 7
The following changes have also been made to the Dark Auction pool:
- Added Prosecute VI
- Added Execute VI
- Removed Sharpness VI
- Removed Protection VI
Designer's Note: We also made some server size improvements to the Dark Auction back in January as part of this perk.
### ➥ InfiniPot™ (rembugquaglet)
The InfiniPot™ is a new accessory, purchasable from Malik for 5,000 Wither Essence, that will automatically consume Dungeon Potions from your inventory or potion bag when a new Dungeons run begins. If the player does not have a Dungeon Potion, the accessory will automatically purchase their desired tier of potion using coins from their purse or bank.
### ➥ Frog's Spirit (KeepAura)
Purchased from the Forest Essence Shop for 5,000 Forest Essence, the Frog's Spirit is an Ability Scroll that can be used to apply the "Leapfrog" ability to Fig and Helix Boots. Once they have the ability scroll applied, players will be able to double jump while Foraging! Ribbit!
### ➥ More Pity & Phoenix Pet Changes (MashClash)
Phoenix Pets have been made less of a SBXP progression blocker, becoming the game's first SPECIAL/VERY SPECIAL pets. Pets of this tier do not count towards your Pet Score and thus don't give any SBXP.
In addition to this, the following pity meters have been added to this game as a result of this Minister's efforts:
- Special Zealots
- Vanquishers
### ➥ Island Weather (ALAND_)
Weather has been added to several islands throughout SkyBlock! Mild weather occurs every 20 minutes, and grants thematic stats to all players on the island. Every 3rd instance of weather will instead of EXTREME, granting more stats alongside an exclusive bonus:
|Island|Mild Weather|Extreme Weather|
|-|-|-|
|Crimson Isle|Ashfall; +25 Fishing Speed; +5 Combat Wisdom; +2.5 Trophy Fish Chance|Hellstorm; +50 Fishing Speed; +10 Combat Wisdom; +5 Trophy Fish Chance; Trophy Fish are 5% more likely to be Gold or Diamond|
|Spider's Den|Rain; +25 Fishing Speed; +5 Combat Wisdom; +2.5% Sea Creature Chance; Rain Slimes spawn around the island|Thunderstorm; +50 Fishing Speed; +10 Combat Wisdom; +5% Sea Creature Chance; Toxic Rain Slimes spawn around the island|
|Moonglade Marsh|Moonfall; +25 Fishing Speed; +10 Foraging Fortune; +2.5% Sea Creature Chance|Fog; +50 Fishing Speed; +25 Foraging Fortune; +5% Sea Creature Chance; Gain 10% more Forest Whispers|
|Dwarven Mines|Breeze; +100 Mining Speed; +25 Mining Fortune; +25 Fishing Speed|Mist; +250 Mining Speed; +50 Mining Fortune; +50 Fishing Speed; Gain 10% more Mithril Powder|
|Crystal Hollows|Breeze; +100 Mining Speed; +25 Mining Fortune; +25 Fishing Speed|Rockfall; +250 Mining Speed; +50 Mining Fortune; +50 Fishing Speed; Gain 10% more Gemstone Powder|
|Glacite Tunnels|Breeze; +100 Mining Speed; +25 Mining Fortune; +25 Fishing Speed|Snowstorm; +250 Mining Speed; +50 Mining Fortune; +50 Fishing Speed; Gain 10% more Glacite Powder|
|The End|Wispful; +25 Mining Fortune; +1 Tracking; +5 Combat Wisdom|Void Storm; +50 Mining Fortune; +2.5 Tracking; +10 Combat Wisdom; Superior Dragons are 1% more likely to spawn|
|Backwater Bayou|Smog; +25 Fishing Speed; +2.5 Sea Creature Chance; +1 Treasure Chance|Acid Rain; +50 Fishing Speed; +5 Sea Creature Chance; +2.5 Treasure Chance; +20% chance to catch two pieces of Junk at once|
|Lotus Atoll|Tropical Rain; +25 Fishing Speed; +2.5 Sea Creature Chance; +2.5 Trophy Chance|Blossoming; +50 Fishing Speed; +5 Sea Creature Chance; +5 Trophy Chance; Trophy Frogs are 5% more likely to be Gold or Diamond|
|The Garden|Rain; +5 Bonus Pest Chance; +2.5 Overbloom; +25 Farming Fortune|Blooming; +10 Bonus Pest Chance; +5 Overbloom; +50 Farming Fortune; Gain 10% more Sowdust|
|Jerry's Workshop|Breeze; +25 Fishing Speed; +5 Sea Creature Chance; +2.5 Treasure Chance|Blizzard; +50 Fishing Speed; +10 Sea Creature Chance; +5 Treasure Chance; Gain 10% more Ice Essence|
To see a list of all the types of Weather and their accompanying buffs, you can climb the mountain in The Hub and speak to Professor Wynd who, due to a recent crash landing, has taken up residence inside what was previously Scoop's house. He's been studying these strange bouts of weather and would be glad to show you his research. If you obtain his Abiphone Contact, he'll even notify you when weather is active on islands you show interest in.
Designer's Note: We plan on adding Weather to more islands in the future. We may also revisit older islands with pre-existing weather for the purposes of optimization or balancing.
### ➥ Dungeon Tick Changes (FlameOfWar)
Even more Dungeon changes come courtesy of this Minister, who has made changes to secrets that will lead to faster clears on all floors as well as some more specific changes to the "Simon Says" terminal:
- Reduced the amount of time it takes to pick up a secret from 11 → 5 ticks
- Removed one set of lights from the "Simon Says" terminal in F7/M7
### ➥ Related Changes
- Reduced Pandora's Box "Pristine Proc'er" challenge from 200x → 150x Flawed Gemstones
- Reduced the droprate of Refined Mineral/Glossy Gemstone by 5x as a result of Fiestas being yearlong
  - Designer's Note: We will keep an eye on how this impacts prices/progression and will buff if needed.
- Renamed some items:
  - Infinityboom TNT → InfiniBoom™ TNT
  - Infinileap → InfiniLeap™
- Improved itemlore of Refined Minerals
- Improved itemlore of Glossy Gemstones
- Improved itemlore of Magical Milk Bucket
- Adjusted Spinosaurus Pet's "Primordial Fisher" perk, and renamed it to "Adaptive Fisher":
  - Old: Increases this pet’s base stats by x% during rain.
  - New: Increases this pet’s base stats by x% during Weather.
- Adjusted Quartzfang Shard:
  - Old: Mining Speed from Events is increased by +10-100%.
  - New: Grants +10-100 Mining Speed on Mining Islands while Weather is active
- Adjusted category of Implosion, Shadow Warp, Wither Shield from DUNGEON ITEM → ABILITY SCROLL
- Adjusted the location of recipes in the Clay Collection to make room for the Hotspot Accessory line
- Adjusted the Blizzard in a Bottle to now increase the effects of weather on players by 10%
  - Designer's Note: We have made a note to revisit this item at a future date and see if we can find a place for it that better serves its original purpose. However, for the time being, it wasn't compatible with the new Weather system.
- Adjusted Fishing Speed stat scaling to be more linear
  - Designer's Note: These changes look to make Fishing Speed gains more noticeable for early-game fishers instead of ramping up more noticeably as you approach the cap. Nobody at any game stage gets weaker here.
- Adjusted Creative Minds:
  - New Creative Minds are Soulbound
  - The item description can now be customized by Staff to input a reason for awarding
  - Going forward, the descriptions for new Creative Minds will state they were awarded by "The Hypixel Team"
    - Designer's Note: The goal here is for us not to effectively be dumping several billion coins on a player when awarding them a Creative Mind. Like the original Wiki items, CM's are intended to be a commemoration of an achievement, not a marketable asset.
## ► Greenhouse QOL
As previously mentioned in the 0.27.1 update, we promised some QOL changes to the Greenhouse, especially around the Decay and Watering mechanics. We think these changes will make a significant difference to how you engage with the Greenhouse, since you should no longer need to log in at specific times to keep your mutations from decaying or worry about your plants drying up before you come back.
- Changed how the decay mechanic functions inside the Greenhouse:
  - A new value will be attached to mutations you plant inside the Greenhouse, which counts down the minimum amount of mutations it can help create. The value is unique for each mutation, listed below.
  - In order for a mutation to decay now, both the decay timer and and this new minimum mutation value have to reach zero.
  - If the decay timer expires and the minimum mutation value has not been met yet, the decay timer is extended by an additional 24h until the minimum mutation value has been met. This can extend indefinitely if you don't come back.
  - Mutations of the same type have their minimum mutation value combined together to prevent single mutations decaying separately.
    - Designer's Note: The goal with these changes is to remove the need for players to have to log in at specific times of the day in order to make sure their mutations don’t decay. The minimum mutation value aims to solve this issue, by not decaying any of your mutations as long as that minimum has not been hit yet. We also want to make sure mutations don’t die at separate times of the day and need to be replanted separately, which is why we are keeping the old decay timer so that if you set up your Greenhouse all at once, it will tend to decay all at the same time as well.
All Minimum Mutation Values
|Mutation|Mutation Value|
|-|-|
|Wheat|12|
|Carrot|12|
|Potato|12|
|Cocoa Beans|12|
|Melon|12|
|Pumpkin|12|
|Sugar Cane|12|
|Cactus|12|
|Nether Wart|12|
|Mushroom|12|
|Sunflower|12|
|Moonflower|12|
|Wild Rose|12|
|Dustgrain|10|
|Scourroot|10|
|Gloomgourd|10|
|Shadevine|10|
|Choconut|10|
|Veilshroom|10|
|Ashwreath|10|
|Witherbloom|10|
|Lonelily|10|
|Coalroot|10|
|Chocoberry|10|
|Creambloom|8|
|Duskbloom|8|
|Thornshade|8|
|Cindershade|8|
|Chloronite|8|
|Cheesebite|8|
|Soggybud|8|
|Blastberry|8|
|Magic Jellybean|Infinite|
|Noctilume|8|
|Donoeatshroom|8|
|Snoozling|8|
|Fleshtrap|6|
|Chorus Fruit|6|
|Thunderling|6|
|Turtlellini|N/A|
|Shellfruit|6|
|PlantBoy Advance|6|
|Startlevine|6|
|Puffercloud|6|
|Zombud|6|
|Phantomleaf|N/A|
|Stoplight Petal|N/A|
|All-in Aloe|N/A|
|Godseed|N/A|
|Glasscorn|N/A|
|Jerryflower|N/A|
|Devourer|N/A|
|Timestalk|N/A|
|Dead Plant|10|
- Watering has been changed to no longer kill crops/mutations, only halting them until watered. While halted, they won’t be able to spread mutations, share crop effects or count towards the unique bonus
  - Designer's Note: This is a similar principle as the decay change, we want to make sure players don’t feel the need to have to water their plants at specific times. Instead, plants will halt until watered. You can take a break for days or weeks from the greenhouse at a time without issues.
- PlantBoy Advance no longer reduces its growth stage by 3 on fail, it will remain the same stage.
- Stoplight Petal no longer destroys the plant when you fail the minigame, it will remain the same stage.
- Thunderling no longer blows up if overcharged, instead it will halt until discharged.
- Carpenter now also shows the base drop rates of base crops in the Crop Guide
- Dead Plants now also have a decay timer of 72h and a minimum mutation value of 10. If they decay, they leave nothing in their place.
  - Designer's Note: This might seem like an odd change, but because Witherbloom relies on Dead Plants to mutate, we need to make sure this new decaying system also works with this mutation.
- Reduced the amount of unique crops needed to reach the maximum bonus from 12 to 10.
- Nerfed the Growth Speed and Yield given by the Unique Crop Bonus from 30% Growth Speed and 36% Yield to 25% Growth Speed and 25% Yield at max
- Added a new attribute shard “Flora” which grants the effect of 1-10 Unique Crop Bonus in your Greenhouse, obtainable through analyzing Mutations
- Added new ability “Sanity Check” to the Plant Diagnostics Tool which allows you to check which mutations are able to grow in any given spot
- Added a toggle for Mass Analyze to only include your inventory
## ► Mob Level Improvements
Mob Levels within SkyBlock have not historically been very accurate. For example, both the Zealot Bruiser and Kalhuiki Tribe Member are currently Lvl100, despite the Tribe Member having 15,384× more Health and dealing 320× more damage per hit. Thus, Mob Level are ultimately quite arbitrary and don't serve as a remotely accurate indicator of the threat posed by the mob you're looking at.
### ➥ Mob Level Formula
The following formulae have been applied to almost all mobs within SkyBlock. It takes the mob's Health and Damage, puts them into a formula to calculate the Mob Level, and then uses that number to calculate the Skill XP, Coins, and XP Orbs given to the player when they kill the mob.
Mob Level Formula
For those interested, the formulae we decided to use for this project can be found below. We were originally inspired by this 2021 forum post and modified it into the formula you see today, which encompasses not just Mob Levels, but Skill XP, Coins, and XP Orbs, too.
|Mob Category|Mob Level Formula|Skill XP Formula|Coin Formula|XP Orb Formula|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Mob|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*1.5|moblvl/3|moblevel*1.1|
|Miniboss|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|(moblvl*1.5)*5|moblvl*2.5|moblevel*3|
|Boss|LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|(moblvl*1.5)*5|moblvl*2.5|moblevel*3|
|Slayer Miniboss|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|(moblvl*1.5)*TIER|1|moblevel*3|
|Slayer Boss|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*DIFF|1|1|1|
|COMMON Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*7.5|moblvl*0.75|moblvl*1.1|
|UNCOMMON Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*10|moblvl*1|moblvl*1.2|
|RARE Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*12.5|moblvl*1.25|moblvl*1.3|
|EPIC Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*15|moblvl*1.5|moblvl*1.5|
|LEGENDARY Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6|moblvl*20|moblvl*2|moblvl*2.5|
|MYTHIC Sea Creature|(LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5|moblvl*25|moblvl*5|moblvl*5|
Designer's Note:
1. When a Shielded mob's HEALTH is used in a calculation, 1 HP = 100,000 HEALTH
2. Mob Level is always rounded DOWN to 0DP. Minimum Mob Level is 1.
  1. It's very important that players see the level system in effect from the onset. We worked around ensuring that the Graveyard Zombie was Lvl1 and the Zombie Villager was Lvl2, seeing as it's slightly stronger/faster. Rounding down facilitated this goal better than rounding up did.
3. Coins are always rounded DOWN to 0DP. Minimum Coins is 1.
4. XP is always rounded down to 0DP. Minimum Combat XP is 5, minimum Fishing XP is 25.
5. EXP Orbs are always rounded DOWN to 0DP. Minimum XP Orbs is 1.
6. Pests use the parameters for "Mob", except they give a flat 1,500 Farming XP (or 6,000 if Field Mouse)
7. "TIER" corresponds to the tier of Slayer that the Slayer Miniboss is eligible to spawn at.
8. "DIFF" corresponds to a manual difficulty modifier placed upon Slayer Bosses indicative of their difficulty. It exists primarily because some Slayer Bosses, such as the Inferno Demonlord, don't actually have a proper Damage stat, so without manual alteration it would have a far lower Mob Level than other Slayer Bosses/generally difficult mobs:
9. Zombie Slayer uses (LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*1.5
10. Blaze Slayer uses (LOG(HEALTH*DAMAGE)-2)^2.6*3.5
11. Please note that these formulae may be adjusted in the future - they are not final.
As a result of every mob in the game now giving a different amount of Combat/Fishing XP than it used to, we've taken the time to revisit some areas of the game that are particularly reliant on the fine-tuning of Combat XP - Slayers. In most cases Slayers had got faster but, in the areas where they were now slower, we made some improvements. We also adjusted the values of some mobs that we felt were too strong/weak for their place in the game:
### ➥ Zombie Slayer Changes
List of Zombie Slayer Changes
Designer's Note: You'll see a lot of mobs having their stats here adjusted because, on our end, this automatically updates their Combat XP to reflect the difficulty posed by their new stats. We've also increased the Combat XP required to spawn the Slayer Boss because, on Alpha, it was proving to be a bit faster than intended, and adjusted the Ghoul Pet from 1.5x Combat XP → +50 Combat Wisdom on account of the prior multiplier being a remnant of an outdated system. Now that Slayer-related mobs (and mobs throughout the entire game) give more Combat XP in the vast majority of cases, we don't think the 1.5x multiplier is needed.

However, in the interest of not making the early-game more grindy or further expanding the gap between Spider's Den Broodfather spawning and Crimson Isle Broodfather spawning, we have manually input higher Combat XP values for the time being. In the future, though, we would like to go back, improve the Spider's Den experience to ensure players are better geared for the challenge, and raise the HP/DMG stats of mobs in Arachne's Burrow accordingly.

We think nerfing it will make The End → Crimson Isle transition a little less daunting for newer players - if it allows players to spawn bosses at truly ridiculous rates we may just manually set the Combat XP of the Spiders to be lower than 125 in the future.

## ► Reforge Stone QOL
### ➥ Stats Menu
You are now able to right-click any reforge stone to view its stats at all rarities and its reforge bonus via a menu.
### ➥ Reforge Guide
We've added a Reforge Guide accessible through /reforges. Clicking on any of the Reforge Stones in this menu opens their respective stats menu. The guide can also be accessed through The Hex and the Blacksmith's menu.
### ➥ Textures
All Reforge Stones have been given unique textures! Hop in-game to get a better look!
### ➥ Costs
The cost to apply an Advanced Reforge now scales with the Reforge Stone's rarity rather than the item's rarity:
- COMMON: 5,000 Coins
- UNCOMMON: 25,000 Coins
- RARE: 100,000 Coins
- EPIC: 250,000 Coins
- LEGENDARY: 500,000 Coins
- MYTHIC: 1,000,000 Coins
- DIVINE: 2,000,000 Coins
In conjunction with this change, all Basic Reforges now cost only 500 Coins to apply regardless of your item's rarity.
Designer's Note: This change gets rid of the awkwardness that happened when you recombobulated an item before reforging it, meaning you had to pay more Coins than if you did it in the other order.
### ➥ Other Changes
- Added a new "Stiff Bonus" to the Hardened Wood Reforge Stone

- Added a new "Treacherous Bonus" to the Rusty Anchor Reforge Stone

  - Also added some Treasure Chance to each tier and nerfed the Sea Creature Chance given slightly
- Added a new "Salty Bonus" to the Salt Cube Reforge Stone
  - Doubles the stats granted by this Reforge while on Jerry Island
  - Decreased the Sea Creature given at base to account for the new Reforge Bonus
- Moved the stats from the Toil Log's reforge bonus to its base stats
  - Also renamed it to "Rotting Branch"
- Moved stats from the Moil Log's reforge bonus to its base stats
- Added DIVINE rarity stats of all reforges
- A handful of Reforge Stones had bonuses that scaled with item rarity, their scaling has been removed
- Rewrote the descriptions of all reforge bonuses to be more clear and visually appealing
- Changed the rarities of many Reforge Stones in the game
- Item types are now colored properly in the descriptions of Reforge Stones
- Reforge Stones can no longer be recombobulated
- Added an easter egg with Sun's Grasp

Reforge Stones aren't the only things getting a glow-up! All pieces of equipment now have some neat new sprites too!

## ► Bug Fixes
- Fixed Goblin Eggs being able to spawn Goblins on islands other than the Dwarven Mines, Crystal Hollows, or Glacite Mineshafts

- Fixed a typo in the description of Rusty Spoon
- Fixed an inconsistency in the descriptions between the Lunar Moth and Firefly Shards
## ► Announcement Roundup
This is not an exhaustive list of things we are working on, just things we are comfortable sharing right now! You can read the previous Announcement Roundup HERE.
### ➥ Magic Find QOL
This update has finished its QA phase and we're looking to get it on the Alpha Network in the near future for prolonged playtesting. You can read more about what we plan to do in this update HERE.
### ➥ Accessory Bag Revamp
We would like to address two questions/concerns that have come up frequently since our initial announcement of this update:
1. Are you removing Jacobus?
  1. No, we are not planning on removing or reworking Jacobus in this update. Your Accessory Bag will still have a limited number of slots. We understand that Jacobus is a pain point for many players, but he plays a key part in helping prevent inflation in the economy. Jacobus is still on our radar, but "nerfing" him would require us to make structural changes to how SkyBlock's economy works so that it's not so dependent on Coins.
  2. On a related note, with this update we will be increasing the default # of Accessory Bag slots the player has to help streamline the new player experience, so in effect everyone will be getting a few free slots with this update.
2. Are you reworking Personal Compactors?
  1. Yes. The current plan is to rework Personal Compactors so that they no longer use slots and will instead allow you to toggle which items in your Sack you would like compacted. We'll also be splitting the single line of Personal Compactors into one for each skill/sack. Each of these skill-specific Personal Compactors will be placed early in a collection for the relevant skill so that new players have access to them regardless of what skill they choose to pursue.
### ➥ Early Game Fishing Improvements
This update has wrapped up development and has now entered the QA phase. Our primary goal with this update is to develop the Fishing Outpost into a more concrete learning experience that provides new players with a few more fundamentals (and some better gear!) before they set sail for the Backwater Bayou, as well as to breathe new life into some old Sea Creatures as part of improvements to Fishing Bait crafting/obtainment.
### ➥ Pale Citadel
Progress has been steady on the Pale Citadel, but we don't want to show off too much yet as we'd like for most of the bosses in the Raid to be a surprise!
If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, please report it HERE!
That's all for today! :hypixel_smile:

# Changelog/2026/September 1
Categories: 2026 Updates, Updates
0.27.1
Hypixel SkyBlock 0.27.1 - Chocolate Factory Improvements, QOL Changes, and more!
Updated: September 1, 2026
Hello! Today we've got a smaller release to Hypixel SkyBlock which includes some new content for the Chocolate Factory, many QOL changes, and bug fixes. We've also got an Announcements Roundup at the end of the thread to let you know what's in store for SkyBlock in the near future.
## ► Chocolate Factory Improvements
### ➥ Rabbit Factions
Starting at Chocolate Factory V, you'll unlock the Faction Tower, where you're able to select one of four Factions: the City Rabbits, the Mountain Rabbits, the Country Rabbits, and the Beach Rabbits. You can change which Faction you are aligned with for 1B Chocolate.
Being part of a Faction means you can find new Rabbits that are exclusive to that Faction. Finding these Rabbits are special, as they don't get automatically added to your collection. Instead, they'll drop as an item that you can trade with other players or buy/sell on the Bazaar. Right-clicking on them consumes the item and adds them to your collection permanently.
Each Faction has 4 levels of dedication that you can level up using Chocolate, the higher the level, the higher the rarity of Faction Rabbits you can potentially find! Each Faction has 5 COMMON, 4 UNCOMMON, 3 RARE, 2 EPIC, and 1 LEGENDARY Chocolate Rabbit to find. Note that Faction Rabbits are twice as rare as Chocolate Rabbits of the same rarity.
### ➥ Chocobits
Starting at Chocolate Factory VI, you'll unlock the Chocobit Tower, which allows you to find SPECIAL Chocobit Rabbits that no one else in SkyBlock can have at the same time! There are 33,333 Chocobits total and they drop twice as frequently as MYTHIC Rabbits, and they each add 0.2x to the multiplier of your Chocolate Factory! Note that Chocobits are not stored in your normal Chocolate Rabbit Collection and they do not take up space.
...but there's a catch! You must pay 1M Coins each SkyBlock Year per Chocobit you own, or else it'll be released back into the pool for anyone to potentially get! When a Chocobit gets released, they'll give you two SkyBlock Years worth of Chocolate production that they would've made otherwise as a thanks!
If you already have every DIVINE Chocolate Rabbit, the pity for it will be replaced with pity for Chocobits.
### ➥ Other
- Doubled the drop rates of MYTHIC and DIVINE Rabbits
  - With this change their Pity amounts have been reduced to 250 and 1,250 respectively
- Added a new Chocobun Shard that can be fused into with Bunbun and any Crime Family shard
  - Adds +1 Level Cap to Rabbit Bro, Cousin, and Sis per level
- You can now buy 3 Dark Cacao Truffles per SkyBlock Year instead of only 2
- Buffed the Rabbit Crew Attribute from 2 ➜ 20 to 5 ➜ 50 Chocolate per second
- Increased Max Chocolate for certain Chocolate Factory levels
  - Level 3: 4B → 5B
  - Level 4: 10B → 25B
  - Level 5: 30B → 50B
  - Level 6: 60B → 100B
## ► QOL Changes
### ➥ Fishing
- Reduced the number of Rusty Coins needed to purchased a Challenging Rod from 16x to 8x
- The Travel Scroll to the Backwater Bayou is now earned in Chapter II for the Backwater Bayou (it is no longer in Junker Joel's shop)
  - More scrolls can be purchased from Rusty for 25,000 Coins
  - Researcher Gilbert now asks for a Busted Belt Buckle instead of a Travel Scroll to the Backwater Bayou
- The Travel Scroll to the Lotus Atoll is now earned in Chapter II for the Lotus Atoll (it is no longer a Trophy Fishing reward)
  - More scrolls can be purchased from Rusty for 50,000 Coins
- Corruption Enchantment changes
  - No longer drops from Corrupted Sea Creatures
  - Corrupted I books are now crafted using 64x Corrupted Fragments and 3x Enchanted Paper
- Rebalanced how Sea Creatures on the Lotus Atoll drop Lotus
  - The Atoll Croaker and Lotus Guardian now only drop 1x and 2x Lotus respectively (nerf)
  - gorF now drops x8 Lotus (buff)
  - The Drowned Captain now drops 32x Lotus with a 20% chance to also drop 1x Silver Lotus (buff)
  - Increased the drop rate of Gold Lotus from Puddle Jumpers from 0.5% to 1%
  - The Frog Prince now always drops 8x Silver Lotus (buff)
  - Increased the drop rate of Gold Lotus from the Frog Prince from 2.5% to 5%
- You now only need to reach Lotus Collection VII for Chapter V in the Lotus Atoll
- Increased the drop rate of Frogcoins from Lotus Guardians from 1% to 5%
### ➥ Combat
- Capped the bonus from the counter on the General's Medallion to 5 digits instead of 6
  - You'll now only need 10,000 Secrets to max it out instead of 100,000
- Reduced the requirements needed for Chili Pepper Collection V-IX
  - You'll now only need 10,000x Chili Peppers to max out this collection
- Increased the drop rate of Flaming Fists from Barbarian Duke X from 0.2% to 0.5%
- The Bingo Combat Talisman now gives +5 Global Wisdom
  - It has also been renamed to the Bingo Wisdom Talisman
### ➥ Other
- Added Hemovibe, Hemoglass, and Hemobombs to the Slayer Sack
- Reduced the number of Hemobombs needed to craft the Blood Donor Artifact from 128x to 32x
- Reduced the number of Nearly-Whole Carrots needed to craft Exportable Carrots from 2x to 1x
- Reduced Hemovibe Collection max tier from 250,000 -> 100,000 Hemovibe
- Tripled the drop rate of Rock the Fish from Dark Monoliths
- Rescaled the number of logs needed to max out the Absorb enchantment
  - You'll now only need 25,000,000 Logs to max it out instead of 50,000,000
- Added Arachne's Calling, Arachne Fragments, and Soul String to the Combat Sack
- Added Enchanted Gunpowder drops to some mobs:
  - Sneaky Creeper (0.5%)
  - Fireproof Witch (5%) (Also now always drops 8x Gunpowder
- Reworked the Pig Brain Mixin to now just give a flat 10% chance to receive an extra item from Shiny Pigs
## ► Other Changes
- Added a DPS Meter above Training Dummies
- Improved the responsiveness of F7 Terminals for players with high ping ("pingless terminals")
- Arrow cost changes
  - Reinforced Iron Arrow: 24x Iron Ingot ➜ 1x Enchanted Iron Ingot
  - Gold-Tipped Arrow: 40x Gold Ingot ➜ 1x Enchanted Gold Ingot
  - Redstone-Tipped Arrow: 24x Block of Redstone ➜ 1x Enchanted Redstone Dust
  - Emerald-Tipped Arrow: 16x Block of Emerald ➜ 1x Enchanted Emerald
  - Bouncy Arrow: 24x Slime Block ➜ 2x Enchanted Slimeball
  - Icy Arrow: 64x Packed Ice ➜ 2x Enchanted Ice
  - Explosive Arrow: 24x Block of Coal ➜ 2x Enchanted Gunpowder
  - Glue Arrow: x64 Tarantula Web ➜ 2x Enchanted String
  - Regular Arrows are now cheaper from the Weaponsmith and Ophelia, only costing 5 Coins per
  - Increased the amounts given for each Arrow in their more trading options menu by x5
- Reduced the drop rate of Ananke Feathers by 25% from Kuudra
- Added the Greenhouse to the Enchanted Clock
- The Coin cost for removing Rod Parts now scales with the Rod Part's rarity
  - COMMON: 10,000 Coins
  - UNCOMMON: 25,000 Coins
  - RARE: 50,000 Coins
  - EPIC: 100,000 Coins
  - LEGENDARY: 250,000 Coins
  - MYTHIC: 500,000 Coins
  - DIVINE: 1,000,000 Coins
  - Along with this change the rarities of many Rod Parts have been changed
  - You can also no longer Recombobulate Rod Parts as a result of this change
- Added text to the item lore of Flexbones explaining where to obtain them
- Mango Dye is now dropped from Tree Gifts
  - 1/1M from Fig Tree Gifts
  - 1/500k from Mangrove Tree Gifts
  - 1/100k from Helix Tree Gifts
- Added 10 Crit Damage to the base stat of Manticore Claws
- The Bestiary entry for Shiny Pigs now displays Year of the Pig loot
  - We also added Enchanted Cooked Porkchops as a rare drop from them (2500 weight)
- Improved shift-clicking items out of the Crafting Table
- Improved shift-clicking items out of the Brewing Stand
## ► Bug Fixes
- Fixed Lumber Jack, Kelly, Ryan, Melody, and Molebert all accepting stacks of logs for their quest only if you had the exact amount in your inventory
- Fixed a way to bypass the Inferno Slayer boss's Magic Damage reduction
- Fixed the Tormentor ability using the damage of the original hit instead of recalculating for each chained mob
- Fixed the Brewing Stand not allowing item stacks to be dragged within the inventory
- Fixed a typo in the Burning Vengeance ability's description
- Fixed Aspect of the Dragon working on the Conjoined Brood
- Fixed Ice Spray working on the Conjoined Brood
- Fixed the Knockback enchantment working on the Conjoined Brood
- Fixed being able to damage the Conjoined Brood without first unlocking Tier 5 Spider Slayer
- Fixed Tier 5 Spider Slayer boss loot not being properly loot shared
- Fixed Duplex Arrows not correctly damaging the Spider Boss's egg sacs
- Fixed Pumpkin Minions overproducing while offline
- Fixed waterlogged blocks disappearing in the lobby when filling a bucket
- Fixed players falling through farmland when jumping on it
## ► Announcements Roundup
### ➥ Regarding Minister Perks
As we announced on the 0.27.1 Release Candidate thread, the Ministers update is planned to come out a little later. We've been in contact with Ministers to make sure that they are happy with their addition/change to the game. We're currently in the process of reworking several perks that the Ministers have asked us to.
### ➥ Regarding Mob Level Improvements
We have decided that Mob Level Improvements needs a little bit more adjusting before it's ready to release. Please continue leaving any feedback you have regarding the mob level changes on the 0.27.1 Release Candidate thread.
### ➥ Regarding Foraging
Though feedback to Torrhus Canyon has been great, we have heard the community's concerns that Foraging currently feels like quite an isolated skill that, outside of Hunting, doesn't tie in with the rest of the game. We'd like to formally acknowledge these concerns and let the community know that we have concrete plans for ensuring Foraging's resources are used throughout the rest of the game.
### ➥ Regarding Greenhouse
We would like to take a moment and address some of the concerns about the most recent patch to the Greenhouse on the 20th of August and also talk about a few follow-up changes we have planned.
One of the biggest issues for us going into that patch was Ashwreaths. It’s a COMMON Mutation, and in the 7 days before the change it accounted for just under 25% of all Mutation income. It was way too easy to maintain and it was giving way too many Coins for what it is, so we didn’t really have another choice besides nerfing it. Phantomleaves and Zombuds took up 2nd and 3rd on the podium, which is why they also got nerfed:
Overall, within the seven-day period from August 14th-20th:
- 9.38 Trillion coins were generated, compared to only 6.01T coins being sunk
  - 1.09T was generated from Mutations (11.62% of all coins generated)
  - 3.69T was generated from Active Farming (39.34% of all coins generated)
    - Overall, 50.96% of all coins generated in that seven-day period came from Farming.
In the week after the patch, August 21st-27th, the data was like so:
- 9.15 Trillion coins were generated, compared to only 5.72T coins being sunk
  - 577B was generated from Mutations (6.31% of all coins generated)
  - 3.38T was generated from Active Farming (36.94% of all coins generated)
    - Overall, 43.25% of all coins generated in that seven-day period came from Farming.
Since the August 20th changes, we have observed more diversity in which Mutations players have been growing, something we hoped the balancing changes would induce. We also have some follow-up changes planned:
- Watering and decay will no longer kill plants - only "freeze" them until you return so as to not penalize players for logging in at the wrong time.
- A Mutation spreading mininum will be introduced, which will guarantee you can spread a certain amount of Mutations before you crops decay. More details on this will come later, but we're planning on being quite generous with the mechanic.
We will also be taking a look at the Unique Crop Bonus. Since we added decay to base crops, you currently have to replant them every three days just to keep the bonus, which can get annoying fast. The idea of the Unique Crop Bonus has lost most of its meaning and isn't really used in a healthy manner, especially at the endgame of the Greenhouse. To remedy this, we’re planning on adding a new Attribute Shard you can earn from analyzing mutations. This Shard will let you cover the Unique Crop Bonus without having to keep extra plants in the Greenhouse for it. We’ll also be reducing the bonus from 12 down to 10 and adjusting the values so they make sense to fit in the Shard nicely.
We hope to have these changes out somewhat soon, but we don't have a concrete date to share at this moment.
### ➥ Ashwood Citadel → Pale Citadel
Work in-progress gif, subject to change!
Back in April, we announced that we were working on a new piece of combat content for SkyBlock titled the Ashwood Citadel, now renamed to the Pale Citadel. In the time since then progress has been steady, over half of the bosses and enemies in the raid have been implemented.
### ➥ Magic Find QOL
This previously announced update is wrapping up the QA Phase and we hope to see it on the Alpha Network soon. This will be a prolonged, iterative of Alpha Testing in the same vein as Torrhus Canyon and the Minister Perks.
### ➥ Accessory Bag Revamp
Work in-progress image, subject to change!
We've begun development on a revamp to the Accessory Bag that turns it into more of a Museum-type menu rather than a standard Minecraft inventory. The plan is for each Accessory line in the game to have a dedicated slot in the Accessory Bag. This allows us to add proper sorting/filtering and search features, with the added benefit of making it super easy to see what Accessories you are still missing!
Each slot will tell you where you can find that Accessory within SkyBlock along with if it has any upgrades, a feature that we think will greatly help newer players.
### ➥ Early Game Fishing Improvements
Also in the works are some early-game fishing changes/content that will focus on expanding the Fishing Outpost in The Hub. For new players, this means a more streamlined introduction to pre-bayou fishing, and for veterans it means changes to the bait system that make certain baits more accessible and makes the bulk-crafting of baits significantly easier.
And that's all for today! If you encounter any bugs or unexpected behavior, make sure to report it HERE!

# Necromancy
Aliases: Raise Souls
Categories: Abilities, Necromancy, Mechanics
[Necromancy]
Necromancy is a game mechanic that allows players to absorb souls from slain monsters and summon them.
Killing a mob with a Necromancy item equipped grants a chance of dropping its soul, which can then be summoned to deal and take damage for the player.
## Items
Certain items have the Raise Souls ability, which is necessary in order to obtain and summon souls. To activate the ability with the [[Summoning Ring]], the item must be present in the player's inventory. With the other items, the player must use them to kill the mob they intend to capture its soul with.
|Slot|Name|Capacity|
|-|-|-|
|[[Summoning Ring]]|[[Summoning Ring]]|2|
|[[Necromancer Sword]]|[[Necromancer Sword]]|2|
|[[Reaper Scythe]]|[[Reaper Scythe]]|3|
### Maximum Souls
Certain items can be used to extend the maximum amount of souls the player can store and summon.
|Slot|Name|Increase|
|-|-|-|
|[[Reaper Mask]]|[[Reaper Mask]]|+2|
|[[Necromancer Lord Armor]]|Necromancer Lord Helmet ([[Necromancer Lord Armor]])|+1|
|[[Necromancer Lord Armor]]|Necromancer Lord Chestplate ([[Necromancer Lord Armor]])|+1|
|[[Necromancer Lord Armor]]|Necromancer Lord Leggings ([[Necromancer Lord Armor]])|+1|
|[[Necromancer Lord Armor]]|Necromancer Lord Boots ([[Necromancer Lord Armor]])|+1|
|[[Ghoul Pet]]|[[Ghoul Pet]]|+2|
Thus, the maximum amount of souls possible is 10 considering the player is using a [[Reaper Scythe]] and wearing a [[Reaper Mask]] along with a [[Necromancer Lord Armor|Necromancer Lord Chestplate]], [[Necromancer Lord Armor|Leggings]] and [[Necromancer Lord Armor|Boots]], and has a [[Ghoul Pet]] equipped.
Attempting to use an item that has more souls than the player can deploy at once results in summoning only the first few souls that were stored in the item. For example, if a [[Reaper Scythe]] has five souls, and the player does not have any armor pieces that boost the Scythe's capacity, then only the first three souls will be summoned.
## Mob Souls
### Mobs
- [[Necromancy/List of Souls|Tab 1]]
### Soul Drop Chance
The drop chance of a soul is determined by the level of the [[Mob]] it drops from, with this formula:
DropChance=max⁡(100−MobLevel,1)100
The drop chance cannot go below 1%. For example, a [Lv1] Mob would have a 99% chance to drop its soul, a [Lv50] Mob would have a 50% chance, and a [Lv200] Mob would have a 1% chance.
### Mana Cost
The following formula is the [[Intelligence|Mana Cost]] required to summon the player's captured mobs:
Damage50+Health100,000
This can be reduced in several ways, calculated multiplicatively.
|Slot|Name|Increase|
|-|-|-|
|[[Sheep Pet]]|[[Sheep Pet]]|-0.1% → -20%|
|[[Wise Dragon Armor]]; [[Wise Dragon Armor]]; [[Wise Dragon Armor]]; [[Wise Dragon Armor]]|[[Wise Dragon Armor]]|-33%|
|[[Ultimate Wise]]; [[Ultimate Wise]]; [[Ultimate Wise]]; [[Ultimate Wise]]; [[Ultimate Wise]]|[[Ultimate Wise]]|-10% → -50%|
|[[Wither Spectre Shard]]|[[Wither Spectre Shard]] Breeze ([[Attributes]])|-1% → -10%|
## Trivia
- The highest damaging mobs currently are the [Lv123] [[Sniper]] for ranged combat and the [Lv100] [[Golem|Woke Golem]] for melee. Alternatively, [MM] [[Tank Zombie]] souls used to be quite cheap and considerably strong and were generally used against [[Voidgloom Seraph]].
  - Now, [[Wither Skeleton]], [[Wither Spectre]] and normal [[Tank Zombie]] souls are considered the top tier necromancy souls against the [[Voidgloom Seraph]].
- The [Lv50] [[Goons|Goon]] souls were only obtainable during the [[Dante]] boss fight for around 1 minute, making them one of the rarest summons.
- Rare souls, such as mini bosses, have a darker shade of purple and a glowing skull when the player obtains their soul on the ground.
- Mobs summoned with necromancy items will despawn after 30s if the summoning player is AFK, and cannot be re-summoned until the player moves.
- Due to [[Derpy]]'s DOUBLE MOBS HP!!! perk, whenever they are elected, all summon mobs' [[Health]] are also doubled.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Added Necromancy.|
|Aug 27, 2022|Summoned undeads are now vulnerable to Slayer Bosses' all attacks including AoE damage.|

# Aranya
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Crimson Isle
[Aranya]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Gives the player a [[Chyme]] if they bring another player (or themselves) with no [[Armor]] on.
Location: [[The Wasteland]]
Coordinates: -322.5 152 -1007.5
Aranya is a spider [[NPC]] found within a cave in [[The Wasteland]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Quest
Aranya will make a deal with the player, stating that if they bring her a player with no [[Armor]] on, she will eat them, and in return the player will receive one [[Chyme]]. The player can sacrifice themselves, but they will not receive any [[Chyme]] for doing so. This can only be done once a week.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Aranya: Welcome to my Den! [NPC] Aranya: I'm a spider, I eat humans, I'm evil, etc... Just bear with me. [NPC] Aranya: I can't eat people wearing armor like you are. [NPC] Aranya: So why don't we make a deal? [NPC] Aranya: You bring me a delicious meal, one without any armor. I'll take them out and you get a reward! [NPC] Aranya: What say you? Are you in?|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Answering Call|[NPC] Aranya: ✆ Hey you! Psst! [NPC] Aranya: ✆ I'm hungry again and I need a meal. [NPC] Aranya: ✆ Could you come help me out at my Den? And bring a friend.; )|
|Eating [[Player]] Other [[Player]]|[NPC] Aranya: Yummy yummy in my tummy! [NPC] Aranya: What a feast! [NPC] Aranya: You are quite unscrupulous! Maybe we can be friends!|
|Eating [[Player]] Oneself|[NPC] Aranya: I mean that works too!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|Eating [[Player]]|[NPC] Aranya: Nom nom nom.|
|Eating [[Player]]|[NPC] Aranya: Mmmm, mmm, MMMMM!|
|Before Bringing [[Player]]|[NPC] Aranya: I'll give you something pretty useful. You'll see.|
|Before Bringing [[Player]]|[NPC] Aranya: It has to be a human, and they cannot be wearing any armor. That's all I'm asking!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Aranya: ✆ I am famished! Bring me a meal and I will reward you!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Aranya: ✆ Bring me a human meal. No armor! Thank you!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Aranya: ✆ Hey, just the one I needed to hear from! [NPC] Aranya: ✆ I'm hungry again and I need a meal. [NPC] Aranya: ✆ Could you come help me out at my Den? And bring a friend.; )|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Phone call|✆ Aranya ✆ ✆ RING... [PICK UP] ✆ RING... RING... [PICK UP] ✆ RING... RING... RING... [PICK UP] [NPC] Aranya: ✆ Hey you! Psst! [NPC] Aranya: ✆ I'm hungry again and I need a meal. [NPC] Aranya: ✆ Could you come help me out at my Den? And bring a friend.; )|
|After eating a player|[NPC] Aranya: Maybe you can lure another unsuspecting meal when I'm hungry again? [NPC] Aranya: I dunno, just a though.|
|After eating a player|[NPC] Aranya: I'm full!|
|After eating a player|[NPC] Aranya: I was famished! Thanks!|
## Trivia
- If a player feeds another to Aranya, it will result in the following death message: ☠ <player> was eaten by Aranya with help from <player 2>.
  - If there is no player nearby, the message will instead read: ☠ <player> was eaten by Aranya.

Location: [[Forgotten Skull]]
Coordinates: -373.5 113 -1036.5
Captain Ahone is an [[NPC]] found throughout the [[Crimson Isle]] primarily during the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]], as well as at the end of both [[Factions|Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questlines]].
## Quest
Captain Ahone first appears during the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]] when the player is sent to the [[Mage Council]] to meet about stolen research.
Later in the quest, he appears in the [[Burning Desert]] with a [[Backpack|Small Backpack]], after [[Dean]] tasks the player with finding the lost [[Apprentice (Crimson Isle)|Apprentice]]. Their [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact gets added to the player's Abiphone, instructing them to call upon finding the Apprentice. When this happens, he then walks up to the Apprentice with [[Grelius]] and [[Krevius]], splashes him with healing potions, and then disappears, tasking the player player with reporting back to [[Dean]].
Towards the end of the quest, [[Dean]] tasks the player with calling Captain Ahone, who then instructs the player to meet them by the [[Forgotten Skull]]. He can then be found there with several other [[NPC|NPCs]], who all agree to investigate [[Kuudra]]. This ending also occurs at the end of the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]].
After defeating [[Kuudra]] for the first time as part of either quest, they then appear in the [[Forgotten Skull]] with [[Ugo]] and [[Elle]], disappearing after you continue the quest.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|During the [[Mage Council]] meeting|[NPC] Dean: Oh, <player> is here! They're the one who retrieved the research! [NPC] Queen Nyx: I see, we owe you adventurer, this object is of great value to us. [NPC] Dean: Yes indeed! With it we are able to extract salt from Netherrack, it will be a massive asset when cooking. [NPC] Dean: You see, while we evolved past the need for crops and cattle, the quality of our food remains low. [NPC] Dean: I would say close to 50% of our research is still spent on food artifacts and machines. [NPC] Captain Ahone: And once again those Piglins stole our research. [NPC] Captain Ahone: They believe we are working on war engines. [NPC] Captain Ahone: The tension is rising and soon we might have to cross swords with them. [NPC] Queen Nyx: Captain Ahone, this isn't a conversation we should have right now. [NPC] Queen Nyx: Thank you very much, <player>. Would you mind leaving us now, we have matters to discuss. [NPC] Dean: <player> please meet me back at the Academy, we have a lot to talk about!|
|In the [[Mage Council]], after the meeting|[NPC] Captain Ahone: Our relations with the Piglins is getting worse, things are spiraling out of control.|
|First Interaction in the [[Burning Desert]]; After Being Sent by [[Dean]]|[NPC] Captain Ahone: You are looking for the Dean's Apprentice? [NPC] Captain Ahone: I'm afraid he might be in great danger, I just found his backpack and there is some blood on it... [NPC] Captain Ahone: We must find him at once, I really hope this isn't the Piglin's doing. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Let's separate. Take my contact and call me if you find anything. ✆ Captain Ahone has been added to your Abiphone's contacts!|
|Looking for the Apprentice ([[Apprentice (Crimson Isle)]])|[NPC] Captain Ahone: He might be nearby, let me know if you find anything.|
|Calling with an Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]); After Finding Apprentice|[NPC] Captain Ahone: ✆ You found the Apprentice? [NPC] Captain Ahone: ✆ Good work. We will come to you at once!|
|After Help Arrives|[NPC] Captain Ahone: Looks like we found him just in time, he will live, but he needs medical attention. [NPC] Captain Ahone: I know the Dean's Apprentice, they would never take that much damage from the monsters around here. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Once they wake up from their coma we will know what happened to them, but I have my suspicions. [NPC] Captain Ahone: The guards and I will bring him back to the city, please explain the situation to the Dean.|
|After giving [[Dean]] his resources; (Can appear as Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) dialogue)|[NPC] Captain Ahone: <player>! Perfect timing [NPC] Captain Ahone: Our entire stash of Sulphur got stolen. I have no idea how "they" did it. [NPC] Captain Ahone: The Queen is warning me against hasty conclusions, but enough is enough. [NPC] Captain Ahone: We cannot let the Barbarians trespass our borders without retaliation. [NPC] Captain Ahone: My scouts told me they sighted Barbarian activities in the North, near the Forgotten Skull. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Let's meet again there, and be prepared for combat.|
|Before heading to the [[Forgotten Skull]]; (Can appear as Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) dialogue)|[NPC] Captain Ahone: The Forgotten Skull in the North is close to the Barbarian borders, they are probably hiding the stolen Sulphur there.|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Captain Ahone: Some matters cannot be solved peacefully.|
|During [[Forgotten Skull]] Meeting|[NPC] Elle: Captain Ahone, Ugo, this is a misunderstanding! [NPC] Elle: I saw what happened! I saw everything! [NPC] Elle: A monstruous[sic] creature was carrying the Sulphur, mountains of Sulphur! [NPC] Elle: The monster had giant tentacles, I have never seen anything like it before in my life! [NPC] Captain Ahone: Elle of the Nether, do not attempt to trick us. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Your motives are noble, but time for diplomacy is over. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Barbarians, return our Sulphur at once, or face the consequences of your actions. [NPC] Ugo: WE stole YOUR Sulphur?! You wizards have some nerves! [NPC] Ugo: We came here following the trails of the Sulphur that YOU robbed! [NPC] Elle: ENOUGH! [NPC] Elle: You were BOTH robbed, isn't it obvious?! [NPC] Elle: The culprit is inside, and it is not Human nor Piglin. [NPC] Elle: I have no idea what it is, but I have to warn you, that fiend is immensely powerful. [NPC] Elle: Now if you wish to retrieve your Sulphur, follow me inside. [NPC] Captain Ahone: This giant tentacle monster story is real? [NPC] Captain Ahone: I have trouble believing it, but in any case we have to get to the bottom of this. [NPC] Ugo: I agree, let's get inside.|
|After defeating [[Kuudra]]|[NPC] Elle: What a fight! [NPC] Ugo: And we got all the Sulphur back! [NPC] Ugo: But what exactly was that monster? [NPC] Captain Ahone: No idea, such a titan appearing out of nowhere isn't a good sign. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Especially if it's after our Sulphur. [NPC] Ugo: We clearly hurt it real bad, but for all we know it's still alive. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Barbarians, I would like to apologise for accusing you. You fought well out there. [NPC] Ugo: No hard feelings, I also judged you too quickly. [NPC] Ugo: You too <player>, you and your friends fought bravely, we couldn't have done it without you [NPC] Captain Ahone: I have to report to the Queen, there is no time to waste. [NPC] Ugo: I'll also alert our Chief. Time to go. [NPC] Elle: Is anyone going to thank me?|
|Calling with an Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]); After defeating [[Kuudra]] and then speaking to [[Dean]]|[NPC] Captain Ahone: ✆ Thank you for helping with the tentacle monster. [NPC] Captain Ahone: ✆ We owe the Piglins an apology. [NPC] Captain Ahone: ✆ I will stay in Scarleton and prepare for the next battle!|

# Carrolyn
Aliases: Carolyn, Exportation
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Carrolyn]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Requests 3,000 of various crops in exchange for various stat bonuses.
Location: [[Scarleton]]
Coordinates: 1 103.0625 -803
Carrolyn is an [[NPC]] located in [[Scarleton]]. The player can give them 3,000 of certain items to gain +12 [[Crop Fortune]] for the matching crop, +5 [[Health]], and [[SkyBlock Levels|+5 SkyBlock XP]] each.
## Items
|Items Required|Reward|
|-|-|
|3,000 (46 stacks plus 56) [[Exportable Carrots]]|+12 Carrot Fortune ([[Crop Fortune]]); +5 [[Health]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|3,000 [[Expired Pumpkin]]|+12 Pumpkin Fortune ([[Crop Fortune]]); +5 [[Health]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|3,000 [[Supreme Chocolate Bar]]|+12 Cocoa Beans Fortune ([[Crop Fortune]]); +5 [[Health]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|3,000 [[Fine Flour]]|+12 Wheat Fortune ([[Crop Fortune]]); +5 [[Health]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|3,000 [[Half-Eaten Mushroom]]|+12 Mushroom Fortune ([[Crop Fortune]]); +5 [[Health]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|3,000 [[Warty]]|+12 Nether Wart Fortune ([[Crop Fortune]]); +5 [[Health]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|3,000 [[Prickly Kiss]]|+12 Wild Rose Fortune ([[Crop Fortune]]); +5 [[Health]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|3,000 [[Potted Cactus]]|+12 Cactus Fortune ([[Crop Fortune]]); +5 [[Health]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

## Trivia
- Carrolyn used to give [[Foraging Fortune]] on [[Carrot|carrots]] instead of the expected [[Farming Fortune]].
- Carrolyn will thank the player but not take the items if they give beyond the first 3,000 of an exportable item.

# Clerk Seraphine
Aliases: Mayor Seraphine, Seraphine
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Mayor
[Clerk Seraphine]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
[Locations]

Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: -1.5 79 10.5
Description: Explains how Mayor Elections ([[Mayor Election]]) work.
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: 4.5 49 34.5
Clerk Seraphine, previously known as Mayor Seraphine or simply Seraphine, is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Community Center]]. She explains how [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]] work and was a key figure in [[Resistance Movement|The Uprising]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None; [[Community Center]]|[NPC] Clerk Seraphine: Welcome to the Community Center! [NPC] Clerk Seraphine: Contribute to community projects, upgrade your account, and more by talking to Elizabeth!|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Clerk Seraphine: Welcome to the Election Room! [NPC] Clerk Seraphine: Is there anything I can help you with? Select an option: ➜ [How are perks chosen?] ➜ [What's the deal with Ministers?] ➜ [What are Special Mayors?] ➜ [I think I'm good.]|
|Selecting [How are the perks chosen?]|[NPC] Clerk Seraphine: Well, most of the Mayors have a total of 4 perks to choose from, the amount they have at once varying. [NPC] Clerk Seraphine: They'll usually have somewhere from 1-3, with an 8% chance to receive a 4th if they already have 3. [NPC] Clerk Seraphine: Candidates that were either elected as Mayor or Minister the previous Election sit out the following Election and only have a single perk when they return. [NPC] Clerk Seraphine: Each Mayor also has a ✯ Minister Perk, which goes hand-in-hand with the Minister system! [NPC] Clerk Seraphine: Do you want to hear more about that? Select an option: ➜ [Sure.] ➜ [I'd rather talk about special Mayors.] ➜ [Nah.]|
|Selecting [What's the deal with Ministers?] / [Sure.]|[NPC] Clerk Seraphine: Each Mayor has a perk marked as a "✯ Minister Perk". [NPC] Clerk Seraphine: That Minister will provide its ✯ Minister Perk as a nice little bonus! [NPC] Clerk Seraphine: Was there anything else you wanted to know about? ## Trivia
- In update 0.8.1, Clerk Seraphine was demoted from her previous position from Mayor to Clerk, in preparation for the [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]].
- She used to be present in the [[Election Room]] during [[Mayor Election|Dante Elections]]. However, she was replaced by [[Clerk Goon]], because it was a mistake made by the admins.
  - When [[Dante]] was elected, Seraphine's spot in the [[Community Center]] and in the [[Election Room]] was replaced with [[Clerk Goon]].

# Crag
Aliases: Kuudra Gatekeeper
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Article stubs, Crimson Isle
[Crag]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]] Recover Sulphur ([[Quests]])
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Allows the player to fight [[Kuudra]].
Location: [[Forgotten Skull]]
Coordinates: -371 114 -1042
Description: Allows the player to fight [[Kuudra]].
Location: [[Dungeon Hub]]
Coordinates: -47.5 121 -24.5
  Not to be confused with [[Chocolate Rabbits|Crag]], a Mountain Faction Rabbit.
Crag is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Forgotten Skull]] and [[Dungeon Hub]]. They appear after defeating [[Kuudra]] for the first time. They let the player select the tier of [[Kuudra]] they would like to fight.
## Purpose
Kuudra Fight Requirements:
|Tier|Requirements|
|-|-|
|Basic|Have [[Elle]] take you to [[Kuudra]]|
|Hot|Complete main quest 1000 Reputation ([[Factions]]) Beat previous tier|
|Burning|3000 Reputation ([[Factions]]) Beat previous tier|
|Fiery|7000 Reputation ([[Factions]]) Beat previous tier|
|Infernal|12000 Reputation ([[Factions]]) Beat previous tier|

# Cult of the Fallen Star
Aliases: Brarnas, Cult, Cult Of The Fallen Star, Cult Of the Fallen Star, Dalir, Fallen Star Cult, Star Cult, Thondin
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dwarven Mines, Locations
Fallen Star Meeting

[Cult of the Fallen Star]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Serves as a member of the Cult of the Fallen Star.
Location: [[Cliffside Veins]]
Coordinates: -44.5 192 47.5
Type: [[NPC]]

Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Serves as a member of the Cult of the Fallen Star.
Location: [[Cliffside Veins]]
Coordinates: -47.5 192 44.5
The cult in the game
The Cult of the Fallen Star is a secret cult found in the [[Cliffside Veins]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]] at [[Coordinates|-45, 193, 45]]. To find the cult, players need to arrive between 0 AM and 6 AM in-game time on either the 7th, 14th, 21st or 28th day of each SkyBlock month. The entrance to the cult's cave is at [[Coordinates|-26, 198, 40]]. After going to a meeting, the player will obtain the [[Achievements|"The Cult of the Fallen Star" Achievement]].
After speaking to [[Cult of the Fallen Star|Brarnas]] for the first time, players will be given the [[Fallen Star Helmet]]. In subsequent meetings with the cult, when the player wears the [[Fallen Star Helmet]], they will receive 200 (3 stacks plus 8) [[Starfall]].
[[Silnar]] is also a member of the Cult, who gives the player a [[Fallen Star Lozenge]].
## Location
The cult's cave is located in a hidden area of [[Cliffside Veins]]; specifically, it is west of the purple crystal and up the cliff. Players can use the lava channel in the [[Lava Springs]] to access the location without having to use ender pearls.

One blue pizza, please. Yup, thanks, we'll come over now, bye! [NPC] Dalir: Alright guys, let's go grab our pizza! And no more talk of all of this XsimulationX stuff, it scares me! [NPC] Thondin: Hopefully it'll all be over when we wake up tomorrow...|
## Gallery

## Trivia
- Meetings, except for the gap between the meetings on the 28th and the 7th, are 7 in-game days apart (2 hours and 20 minutes in real life time).
- Dalir is the only cult member players can interact with.
- Despite the message saying "come back next time" if you forget to wear the helmet, you can simply put on the helmet and then talk to Brarnas again to receive the [[Starfall]].
- The Cult of the Fallen Star is likely the antithesis to the [[Armor of the Rising Sun|Rising Sun]] as they share the same naming scheme.
- There is a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] at [[Coordinates|-52, 205, 48]], which is accessed from their room.
- Thondin states that his favourite food is blue pizza during cult meeting #6.
  - The red and blue pizzas are a reference to The Matrix's red pill and blue pill, where the red pill represents learning an unsettling truth, and the blue pill represents remaining in comfortable ignorance.
- The Cult of the Fallen Star members are one of 5 [[NPC]]'s to mention the "simulation" or the simulated state of the world of skyblock. Others include the [[Royal Resident]], [[Oen]], [[Yoshua]], and [[Skylark]].

# Dean
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Dean]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Mage Faction Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) Join the Mage Faction ([[Quests]]); Purchase an Abiphone ([[Quests]]); Recover Lost Research ([[Quests]]); Find the Dean's Apprentice ([[Quests]]); Help Dean ([[Quests]])
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Serves as one of the main NPCs in the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]).
Location: [[Cathedral]]
Coordinates: -16 123 -882.5
Description: Appears here during the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) to discuss stolen research.
Location: [[Mage Council]]
Coordinates: -121.5 82 -752.5
Dean is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Cathedral]] on the [[Crimson Isle]] and one of the main NPCs in the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]].
## Quest
Dean first appears in the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]] after [[Elle]] tasks the player with giving them an [[Maddox Batphone]]. In return, he grants the [[Letter of Recommendation]], which can be given to [[Queen Nyx]] to join the [[Factions|Mage Faction]].
Not much further into the quest, the player is tasked with calling Dean on an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]], after which he instructs the player to meet him and several other [[NPC|NPCs]] in the [[Mage Council]]. There, they discuss stolen research, which the player must retrieve from inside [[The Bastion]].
After retrieving the research, Dean asks the player to find his lost [[Apprentice (Crimson Isle)|Apprentice]], who is located just north of the [[Burning Desert]]. Upon finding him, he then asks the player to bring him 10 [[Magma Chunk|Magma Chunks]] and 5 [[Burning Eye|Burning Eyes]], which the Apprentice was originally supposed to bring him.
Dean then uses these resources for his work, and in the meanwhile, tasks the player with completing quests for [[Odexar]] and [[Igrupan]]. After this, Dean then asks for 1 [[Hallowed Skull]], 1 [[Lumino Fiber]] and 1 [[Leather Cloth]]. After providing these, the player is tasked with speaking to [[Captain Ahone]], which leads to the player having to defeat [[Kuudra]]. Once this occurs, speaking to Dean again will complete the main questline.

# Dusk

Dusk is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Blacksmith (Zone)|Blacksmith]] within the [[Mining District]] on the [[Hub Island]]. They are found in a room next to the [[Smithmonger]]. Next to them is the [[Runic Pedestal]], which is used for [[Runecrafting]].

|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Dusk: Runes are cosmetic effects that drop from Runic mobs! [NPC] Dusk: Most mobs have a chance to spawn as Runic, so keep your eye out! [NPC] Dusk: You can apply runes to weapons and armor with the Rune Pedestal behind me. [NPC] Dusk: You can also combine two runes for a chance to create a higher level rune with a better effect! [NPC] Dusk: If you're having trouble storing all the runes you've been collecting, you can buy a Rune Sack from Elizabeth in the Community Center!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Dusk: ✆ Shhhh! [NPC] Dusk: ✆ Don't talk so loud. [NPC] Dusk: ✆ I can give you my contact but you need to do something for me. [NPC] Dusk: ✆ Could you kill a Runic Enderman? It HAS to be an enderman. Please.|

Elle is an [[NPC]] on the [[Crimson Isle]]. She acts as a guide, and helps explain the new systems, factions and mobs there.
## Quests
Elle is one of the main [[NPC|NPCs]] for the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]] for both [[Factions]]. She first appears in the [[Stronghold]] trying to fight three [[Blaze (Elle's Quest)|Blazes]], after which she tasks the player with bringing her 4 [[Magma Chunk]], 3 [[Blaze Ashes]] and 2 [[Spectre Dust]]. After the player does this, Elle will start leaving the Stronghold.
She will be in front of the [[Chief's Hut]] within [[Dragontail]], and outside the [[Community Center (Scarleton)|Community Center]] within [[Scarleton]]. She will then task the player with speaking to four [[NPC|NPCs]] from each [[Factions|Faction]], so that they can make their mind up about which one they want to join. The quest then splits depending on which faction was chosen.
Elle is also required to access the Basic Tier of the [[Kuudra]] fight. She will take the player there after they have completed their Faction's entire questline.
