The following dialogue appears when joining or on the [[Factions|Mage Faction]]:
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Joining Mage Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Elle: Hum, so you chose the Mage Faction then. [NPC] Elle: It's not going to be easy to join them. [NPC] Elle: You first have to prove your worth if you want to have a chance at joining their ranks. [NPC] Elle: Hum, let me think... [NPC] Elle: Oh! I know what you should do! [NPC] Elle: The Taika people really like educated individuals. [NPC] Elle: If you can bring them back a book or a piece of technology they have never seen before, I'm sure they will take a liking to you. [NPC] Elle: I couldn't help but notice that you come from the place we commonly know as The Hub. [NPC] Elle: In which case, you must have met Maddox? [NPC] Elle: Both Maddox and his brother are in fact from Scarleton, but contrary to the other inhabitants, the 2 brothers were born without any magical aptitudes. [NPC] Elle: In fact, they instead showed that they had immense strength, and a taste for violence. [NPC] Elle: They got banned from the Academy during their first year, and even got exiled from the island after a terrible incident. [NPC] Elle: But I digress. My point is they are both Taika, and I know for a fact that Aatrox is crafting simple Ability Phones for his brother. [NPC] Elle: In fact I have one myself. Here take it, I'll just request another one from Maddox. I keep losing them.|
|After obtaining the [[Maddox Batphone]]|[NPC] Elle: Bring this to the Dean of the Academy in Scarleton, I'm sure he will find it quite interesting. [NPC] Elle: The Cathedral got reorganized a while ago, now a large portion of it is used for the Academy.|
|After calling [[Dean]]|[NPC] Elle: Oh wow, it worked! I'm so happy for you! [NPC] Elle: And you even got an Abiphone! Take my contact so we can stay in touch. ✆ Elle has been added to your Abiphone's contacts! [NPC] Elle: Nice! So what are you up to? [NPC] Elle: The Dean told you his research got stolen?! [NPC] Elle: Ah, it's not so surprising, maybe you don't know but the Piglins are not so fond of the Taika people. [NPC] Elle: I do have an idea where that stolen research might be, follow me.|
|Arriving outside [[The Bastion]]|[NPC] Elle: You see this giant Dragon Skull inside the Barbarian Faction? [NPC] Elle: That's the skeleton of Jythar the Eternal one - the one who caused the destruction of the world. [NPC] Elle: Now you probably understand why the village in front is called Dragontail. [NPC] Elle: Anyways, the Barbarians built their Bastion inside the skeleton, and I suspect this is where the stolen object is guarded. [NPC] Elle: Piglins fear the growing power of the Taika Kingdom, so they most likely stole it to study the research. [NPC] Elle: Maybe you could sneak in and recover the item. Without getting seen and creating a major diplomatic incident, of course. [NPC] Elle: I'm not sure exactly where they keep it, but just make sure you don't get seen by the guards! [NPC] Elle: I can't help you however, I have way too much to lose sorry. But good luck!|
|Before recovering the stolen research Random|[NPC] Elle: Piglins can't see very far because of the fog, just don't stand in front of them.|
|Before recovering the stolen research Random|[NPC] Elle: Do you even know what you are looking for? Maybe a book or a magical item?|
|After recovering the stolen research|✆ Elle: ✆ ✆ Ring... [PICK UP] ✆ Ring... Ring... [PICK UP] ✆ Ring... Ring... Ring... [PICK UP] [NPC] Elle: ✆ Hello? Oh, you are still alive, awesome! [NPC] Elle: ✆ Have you got the research back or did you chicken out? [NPC] Elle: ✆ Great! You are cooler than you look! [NPC] Elle: ✆ Go back to the Dean then, I'm sure this will grant you a lot of reputation! [NPC] Elle: ✆ Alright then! I have to go now, was nice talking to you!|
The following dialogue appears when joining or on the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]]:

|Joining Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Elle: You want to join the Barbarian Faction, hum. [NPC] Elle: What a strange choice. [NPC] Elle: You either wait for the Volcano to erupt, or you find a Corrupted Monster. [NPC] Elle: And guess what, I know where to look! [NPC] Elle: Follow me!|
|Switching to the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Elle: So you want to join the Barbarian Faction then... [NPC] Elle: I do have an idea how you could do it. [NPC] Elle: Piglins crave Sulphur. Our first goal should be to find some. [NPC] Elle: There are only 2 ways to get Sulphur. [NPC] Elle: You either wait for the Volcano to erupt, or you find a Corrupted Monster. [NPC] Elle: And guess what, I know where to look! [NPC] Elle: Follow me!|
|After Elle spawns a Corrupted [[Flaming Spider]]|[NPC] Elle: Look, we found one! A Corrupted Monster! [NPC] Elle: It's just ahead of us, the Burning Spider! [NPC] Elle: Go fight it, good luck!|
|After killing the Corrupted [[Flaming Spider]]|[NPC] Elle: You handled that quite well. [NPC] Elle: Corrupted Monsters rarely appear on the island, it usually means they consumed too much Sulphur. [NPC] ----: Oink [NPC] Elle: Did you hear that? [NPC] Elle: I think it was coming from here! [NPC] Elle: Let's go.|
|After finding the pig ([[Ugo]])|[NPC] Elle: This Piglin got lost! [NPC] Elle: They probably got cornered by monsters and had to hide here. [NPC] Elle: Piglins turn back into pigs if they go too long without Sulphur. [NPC] Elle: That Corrupted Monster should have dropped some Sulphur. Quick, give it to them!|
|Talking to [[Ugo]] after giving [[Sulphur]]|[NPC] Ugo: You saved me! [NPC] Ugo: Memory is failing me but I think I remember getting attacked. [NPC] Ugo: My leg got hurt and I had to hide here, the rest is blurry... [NPC] Elle: How could you leave Dragontail without Sulphur on you! [NPC] Ugo: I know, I was careless, I was on a mission for... [NPC] Ugo: SULPHUR! My father is going to KILL ME!! [NPC] Elle: Let's not exaggerate. [NPC] Ugo: You don't understand, my father is the Chief! [NPC] Elle: You were not exaggerating. [NPC] Ugo: Please help me, I have to present myself to him. [NPC] Elle: Of course. [NPC] Elle: I will shoulder you to the Chief's Hut. [NPC] Elle: <player> make sure monsters don't attack us on the way there.|
|Whilst [[Chief Scorn]] Scolds Ugo|[NPC] Chief Scorn: Ugo, where have you been? [NPC] Ugo: I- [NPC] Chief Scorn: You had ONE job! [NPC] Chief Scorn: 3 days later you come back with empty hands and a broken leg!? [NPC] Ugo: Well- [NPC] Chief Scorn: And you two, what are your names? [NPC] Elle: I'm- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Thank you for saving my rascal of a son. [NPC] Chief Scorn: Are you looking to join our ranks? [NPC] Elle: No but- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Perfect! [NPC] Chief Scorn: I welcome you both to the Barbarian Faction! [NPC] Chief Scorn: If you betray us you'll die. [NPC] Elle: I can't- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Enough talking! [NPC] Chief Scorn: Ugo, go back out there and finish the job! [NPC] Ugo: But- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Guards, escort them out of my Hut!|
|Talking to [[Ugo]] afterwards|[NPC] Ugo: My leg really hurts. [NPC] Ugo: It was already quite bad before this fall but now... [NPC] Elle: Your father sure has some character. [NPC] Ugo: He's not that bad, you get used to it. [NPC] Elle: I don't know what he wants you to do out there, but we can't let you go back there with your injured leg. [NPC] Ugo: I'm afraid I have to. [NPC] Elle: Just tell us what you have to do and we'll do it for you. [NPC] Ugo: I can't tell you it's highly confidential. [NPC] Elle: I don't care, you can't go back out there with a broken leg. [NPC] Ugo: Sigh [NPC] Ugo: My father believes that the Mage Faction is working on some kind of powerful war instrument. [NPC] Ugo: We know they are hiding something inside their Cathedral. [NPC] Ugo: My job is to infiltrate their Cathedral and steal their war machine so we can study it. [NPC] Elle: Say no more, that's a perfect job for <player>. [NPC] Ugo: Why them? [NPC] Elle: Alright let's go.|
|Arriving Outside [[Cathedral]]|[NPC] Elle: This is the Mage Cathedral. [NPC] Elle: Your job is to enter the Cathedral and find the Dean's office. [NPC] Elle: They are probably hiding the research there. [NPC] Elle: There are multiple entrances, most of them have guards but this one down there is often unprotected. [NPC] Elle: Good luck, I'll be right there in case you need moral support.|
|Before stealing the research|[NPC] Elle: Don't get caught by the guards!|
|Before stealing the research|[NPC] Elle: The Dean's office? I don't know where it is. [NPC] Elle: It's definitely in their Cathedral though, I know they reorganized a lot of it into an Academy.|
|After stealing the research (Calling)|✆ Elle: ✆ ✆ Ring... [PICK UP] ✆ Ring... Ring... [PICK UP] ✆ Ring... Ring... Ring... [PICK UP]|
|After stealing the research; (Can appear as Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) dialogue)|[NPC] Elle: You got the right item? [NPC] Elle: Are you sure? [NPC] Elle: Okay great, let's leave this place. I have Mycelium all over my shoes. [NPC] Elle: Let's talk to Ugo again. [NPC] Elle: I'll use a Travel Scroll, see you there.|

|Upon meeting in the [[Forgotten Skull]]|[NPC] Elle: Captain Ahone, Ugo, this is a misunderstanding! [NPC] Elle: I saw what happened! I saw everything! [NPC] Captain Ahone: Elle of the Nether, do not attempt to trick us. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Your motives are noble, but time for diplomacy is over. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Barbarians, return our Sulphur at once, or face the consequences of your actions. [NPC] Ugo: WE stole YOUR Sulphur?! You wizards have some nerves! [NPC] Elle: The culprit is inside, and it is not Human nor Piglin. [NPC] Elle: I have no idea what it is, but I have to warn you, that fiend is immensely powerful. ## Trivia
- Elle is currently the most animated NPC. She fights mobs in the [[Kuudra]] fights, uses an [[Aspect of the End]] to escape the collapsing bridge cutscene and is one of the NPCs that can walk smoothly.
- Elle uses a Diamond Pickaxe and a Diamond Sword during Kuudra fights.
- If players manage to all die at 100% completion in a Kuudra fight, Elle will still congratulate them as if they had won.
- When Elle requests you to talk to four villagers from each [[Factions|Faction]], only certain ones count, which are indicated by green particles above their head.
  - In [[Scarleton]], these include [[Mage Emissary]], [[Colore]], [[Alwin]], [[Offea]], [[Ulyn]], [[Mollim]], [[Lys]], [[Kheharad]], [[Rhanora]], [[Seffea]], [[Rollim]], [[Ezekiel]], [[Mazakala]], [[Arbadak]], [[Arba]], [[Velyna]], [[Igrupan]], and [[Scholar Alluin]].
  - In [[Dragontail]], these include [[Barbarian Emissary]], [[An]], [[Suus]], [[Turd]], [[Kutral]], [[Nall]], [[Grog]], [[Nuvian]], [[Chihai]], [[Chak]], [[Chuk]], [[Etc]], [[Strux]], [[Baar]], [[Yoink]], [[Pomtair]], [[Truu]], [[Plenk]], [[Bruuh]], [[Kaus]], [[Igor]], [[Porc]], and [[Jine]],

# Fann
Aliases: Hootie, Pet Training
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Fann]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Can level up [[Pets]] after a duration of time.
Location: [[Pet Care]]
Coordinates: 9.5 73 -62.5
Fann is an [[NPC]] who resides in [[Pet Care]] with Hootie, her pet owl. She trains [[Pets]] for a duration of time, leveling them up.
## Overview
Fann trains [[Pets]] for a duration of time, leveling them up. Up to 3 pets may be trained at a time by feeding Hootie 5 [[Great Carrot Candy|Great Carrot Candies]] and 1 [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]] to unlock training slots #2 and #3, respectively.
The cost of leveling up a pet is dependent on the training duration and type. An [[Owl Pet]] can decrease the [[Coins|Coin]] cost by up to 5% and increase the amount of Pet XP earned by up to 10%, and syphoning [[Boreal Owl Shard|Boreal Owl Shards]] can additionally increase the amount of Pet XP earned by up to 20%.
Training can be canceled at any time for free by talking to Fann or by going upstairs in Pet Care and selecting the pet, but doing this only refunds half the remaining training time.
The usage of an [[Enchanted Clock]] can skip 1 hour of the time taken to level up a pet. This ability has a 48 hour cooldown.
## Pet Training
Using higher training types requires progress in the [[Taming]] skill.
| [[Taming]]; Lvl Req.|Training Type|Cost/Day|Pet XP/Day|Pet XP/Day (+30% XP)|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|10|Free||20,000-50,000|26,000-65,000|
|20|Light|200,000 [[Coins]]|250,000|325,000|
|30|Moderate|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|500,000|650,000|
|40|Expert|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|750,000|975,000|
|50|Ultra|2,750,000 [[Coins]]|1,500,000|1,950,000|
|60|Turbo!|3,000,000 [[Coins]]; 500 [[Bits]]|2,500,000|3,250,000|

[NPC] Fann: For a small fee, we'll train your pets and help them while you're off doing heroic things. [NPC] Fann: Hootie approves, trust me! |Feeding Hootie [[Bat the Fish]]|[PET] Hootie: Hoot hoot! Talk to Fann again!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Fann: ✆ My Abiphone contact, you say? [NPC] Fann: ✆ Well, I'm usually a bit guarded about that. [NPC] Fann: ✆ But... [NPC] Fann: ✆ If you treat Hootie to a Bat the Fish, we might just become fast friends!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) After feeding Hootie [[Bat the Fish]]|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Wow, Hootie's absolutely thrilled! [NPC] Fann: ✆ Did you know, Bat the Fish only swims at night? [NPC] Fann: ✆ Alright, you've earned it. [NPC] Fann: ✆ I'll add you to my Abiphone contacts!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Being Called for Pet Level Up|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Hey [player]! [NPC] Fann: ✆ [#[Slot Number]] [Training Type] [Pet] has concluded and is now [Lvl #]|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Fann: This pet I'm looking after is super hungry! You got anything?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Fann: That should work! Hopefully they'll be all tired from eating and finally have a nap.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Fann: Oh my feathers, these little ones are not going to be happy!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Ready for a fun fact? Owls have asymmetrical ears, helping them pinpoint sounds from different directions. Talk about surround sound!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Hoo's ready for some owl wisdom? Owls have been symbols of wisdom in various cultures. Makes me wonder what Hootie's pondering!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Whoo's up for some owl trivia? Did you know owls don't have teeth? They swallow their prey whole. A bit gruesome, but efficient!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Did you know some owls like to take baths? Hootie here loves a good splash now and then!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Listen to this: The Eurasian Eagle-Owl can live up to 60 years in captivity. Imagine all the wisdom they gather!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Listen to this: Some owls have special feathers for silent flight. Perfect for sneaking up on snacks, or in Hootie's case, a cheeky cuddle!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Did you know that owls can rotate their heads up to 270 degrees? Hootie's quite the head-turner!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Owls have a third eyelid! It's transparent and helps protect their eyes while they hunt.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Hoo's up for a flight lesson? Unlike most birds, owls make nearly silent flight possible by the structure of their wing feathers!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Fancy some trivia? The Barn Owl has the most extensive range of any owl species. They're world travelers, just like you!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Fancy this: The smallest owl in the world is the Elf Owl, about the size of a sparrow. Hootie here thinks they're adorable!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Did you know that owls can't chew? That's why they regurgitate pellets with the parts they can't digest!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Hoo knew? Owls can actually swim! Though Hootie prefers to keep his feathers dry, thank you very much!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Brace yourself: Some owls have what's called a ‘facial disc,' a feather arrangement that helps funnel sound to their ears. It's like their own personal satellite dish!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ You'll love this: Owls have zygodactyl feet, meaning two toes face forward and two face backward. Perfect for gripping prey!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Here's a tidbit: Owls have hollow bones, which helps them stay lightweight for flying. It's like nature's own aviation engineering!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Hold onto your feathers! Some owls have a wingspan that's nearly twice their body length. Talk about aerodynamics!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Here's a beak-blowing stat: The Great Gray Owl can locate prey under two feet of snow and snatch it up without a sound!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Have you heard? The Northern Hawk Owl can spot prey from up to half a mile away! That's some keen eyesight!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Here's something: Owls don't have crops, those pouches some birds have for storing food. They're all about that direct-to-stomach service!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Owl Facts|[NPC] Fann: ✆ Did you know the largest owl is the Blakiston's Fish Owl? Its wingspan can reach up to 6.6 feet! Hootie's a bit envious.|
## Trivia
- Disabling Calling Services will cause Fann to show up as Do Not Disturb enabled, even if the player's Abiphone does not have that ability.
- Despite being able to gain Pet XP through other traditional means, the Owl Pet cannot be trained by Fann because selecting it puts it in its respective slot, and trying to input another Owl Pet into the training slot gives the error message: You already have an item in that slot!
## Gallery
- Fann and Hootie as seen in Pet Care.

# Feast Chef Ted
Aliases: Ted
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Feast Chef Ted]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Introduces the player to the [[Harvest Feast]] and Grand Feast ([[Harvest Feast]]) events, and which crops are in-season as part of it.
Location: [[Communal Stew]]
Coordinates: 84.5 72 -78.5
Feast Chef Ted is an [[NPC]] located at the [[Communal Stew]] and in [[The Garden]]. They inform the player which crops are in-season as part of the annual [[Harvest Feast]] and [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]] events.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction during a [[Harvest Feast]]|[NPC] Feast Chef Ted: Welcome to the Harvest Feast! [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: We're cooking up a massive Communal Stew for everyone in SkyBlock and we need your help to season it! [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: Three crops will be in-season at any given time, harvesting them will help season the stew and earn you exclusive items!|
|First Interaction during a Grand Feast ([[Harvest Feast]])|[NPC] Feast Chef Ted: Welcome to the Grand Feast! [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: We're cooking up a massive Communal Stew for everyone in SkyBlock and we need your help to season it! [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: Three crops will be in-season at any given time, harvesting them will help season the stew and earn you exclusive items! [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: Whenever you get Seasoning you'll also earn a Kernel which my brother Scott can bake into more items!|
|Collecting [[Seasoning]] during Grand Feast ([[Harvest Feast]])|RARE CROP! Seasoning (automatically donated) [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: Thanks for the donation! I've added a Kernel to your purse.|
|Reaching Feast Milestones ([[Harvest Feast]]) during Grand Feast ([[Harvest Feast]])|[NPC] Feast Chef Ted: You've hit a new Feast Donation Tier! [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: Talk to me to claim your Mason Jar and Kernels!|
|First interaction after event ends|[NPC] Feast Chef Ted: That might've been our best feast yet! [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: We're hoping to run another one next Early Autumn!|
|Further interactions after event ends|[NPC] Feast Chef Ted: Check the calendar, there should be another feast scheduled!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Without [[Ted's Contact Baked In Bread]]|[NPC] Feast Chef Ted: ✆ Oh I don't give out my information to just anyone. [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: ✆ Only the Feast's top patrons get that luxury. [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: ✆ Check out my brother Scott's Bakery, I'm sure he'll have something to help you get in contact with me.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling Ted during the Feast ([[Harvest Feast]])|[NPC] Feast Chef Ted: ✆ I heard that <crop> will drop Seasoning more often then the other crops this month...|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Start of a new month during the Feast ([[Harvest Feast]])|[NPC] Feast Chef Ted: ✆ Yo! Just wanted to let you know that <crop>, <crop>, and <crop> are now in their harvesting season! [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: ✆ Farming these crops will earn you Seasonings and unique items! [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: ✆ I heard that <crop> will drop Seasoning more often then the other crops this month... [NPC] Feast Chef Ted: ✆ Catch ya later!|
## Trivia
- Players can get up to 2 [[Feast Flask|Feast Flasks]] more from Feast Chef Ted's last milestone, via the Leftovers perk purchased at [[Doug]].
  - When factoring in Leftovers, up to 10 [[Feast Flask|Feast Flasks]] are available per [[Harvest Feast]]. During the [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]], players can get up to 1,125 [[Kernels]] and 18 [[Feast Flask|Feast Flasks]] from the milestone, on top of any obtained by collecting [[Seasoning]].

# Fred
Aliases: Forge Foreman
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters
[Fred]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to access [[The Forge]] after obtaining their Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) contact.
Location: [[The Forge]]
Coordinates: -2.5 148 -68.5
Fred is an [[NPC]] in [[The Forge]].
## Purpose
Fred can be given 64 (1 stack) [[Foul Flesh]] in return for their [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact. Their contact opens the [[The Forge/UI|Forge Menu]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Fred: These forges have been running at an endermite's pace! [NPC] Fred: My job here is to make sure the lava flows and that the titanium shines. [NPC] Fred: Yep, it's mostly watching other people work!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Fred: ✆ Err, are you sure? [NPC] Fred: ✆ The signal doesn't work too well around here, but suit yourself. [NPC] Fred: ✆ Could definitely use some help, if you are willing to trade. [NPC] Fred: ✆ The Forge doesn't charge users for its fuel, but we're using a mountain of it every day. [NPC] Fred: ✆ I'm a little ashamed to admit it, but we mostly use Foul Flesh nowadays. [NPC] Fred: ✆ We tried a bunch of things but that's been working the best so far. [NPC] Fred: ✆ For a stack of Foul Flesh, I'll give you my personal contact. [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Not Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Fred: ✆ You don't have what I need! Nice try!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Fred: ✆ Thanks, I appreciate it!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Answering Call|[NPC] Fred: ✆ Hey [player]! Your <item> is ready down here at the forge!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Without completing [[9f™ Network]]||
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Without completing [[9f™ Network]]|(no dialogue, forge menu opens)|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Without completing [[9f™ Network]]|[NPC] Fred: ✆ I can't really hear you right now, the signal is really bad! [1]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Without completing [[9f™ Network]]|[NPC] Fred: ✆ I'm not interested in extending my anvil warranty. [1]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Without completing [[9f™ Network]]|[NPC] Fred: ✆ You're breaking up, I CAN'T HEAR YOU! [1]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Without completing [[9f™ Network]]|[NPC] Fred: ✆ What are you saying, the forge is in the omelette??? [1]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Without completing [[9f™ Network]]|[NPC] Fred: ✆ All I hear is crackling, the signal is really bad down here. [1]|
[1] The above dialogue lines will appear with some (randomly chosen) characters unintelligible, for example:
[NPC] Fred: ✆ What are you saying, the forge is in the omelette???
## Trivia
- Forge Foreman is displayed above Fred's head.
- Fred is mentioned in the description of the [[Skeleton Keychain]].
## Bugs
- Fred is currently bugged to not call the player when an item is done forging.

|Oct 19, 2022|0.16|Added Fred.|
|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|Fred will no longer complain about the signal after all the [[9f™ Network]] relays have been repaired.|

# George
Aliases: Pet Sell, Pet sell
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Article stubs, Pets, 0.7.8
[George]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Purchases [[Pets]] from players in exchange for [[Coins]]. Can also increase the player's [[Taming]] level cap.
Location: [[Pet Care]]
Coordinates: 21.5 74 -59.5
George is an [[NPC]] located in [[Pet Care]]. They purchase [[Pets]] from the player in return for [[Coins]]. George is also capable of increasing the player's [[Taming]] level cap.
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] George: Hey there, I'm George the Pet Collector! [NPC] George: I travel the world searching far and wide for the rarest pets I can find! [NPC] George: If you have any pets which need a new home I'd be happy to buy them off you!|
|First Interaction; After reaching Taming L (50) ([[Taming]])|[NPC] George: Ah, a seasoned tamer, I see! Welcome! [NPC] George: You've mastered the art of Taming to level 50, a commendable feat. [NPC] George: You see, I have a penchant for collecting exotic pets. [NPC] George: A hobby that's not as easy for me as it is for a skilled tamer like yourself. [NPC] George: In exchange for your help in expanding my collection, I offer a unique opportunity. [NPC] George: For each rare pet you bring to me, starting with the elusive Epic Rift Ferret, I'll unlock an additional level in your Taming skill. [NPC] George: Why the Rift Ferret you ask? [NPC] George: Ah, the Rift is a place of mystery, one I've longed to explore but alas, have never had the chance. [NPC] George: Bring me this creature from beyond, and let's advance your taming prowess together!|
|Giving MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Enderman Pet]]|[NPC] George: Thank you for bringing me the Mythic Enderman. [NPC] George: Their enigmatic presence and otherworldly aura have always captivated me. [NPC] George: Your Taming level cap has been increased!|
|Giving LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) or better [[Black Cat Pet]]|[NPC] George: Shhhh. Don't disturb the Legendary Black Cat. [NPC] George: I've always been fascinated by their mysterious charm. [NPC] George: Your Taming level cap has been increased!|
|Giving EPIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Rift Ferret Pet]]|[NPC] George: Ah, the Rift is a place of mystery, one I've longed to explore but alas, have never had the chance. [NPC] George: Thank you for bringing me this pet! [NPC] George: Your Taming level cap has been increased!|
|Giving EPIC ([[Rarity]]) or better [[Slug Pet]]|[NPC] George: Oh yes, an Epic Slug. [NPC] George: My garden seems to miss having one around! [NPC] George: Your Taming level cap has been increased!|
|Giving EPIC ([[Rarity]]) or better [[Spirit Pet]]|[NPC] George: Is that a Epic Spirit? [NPC] George: Their mysterious aura has always intrigued me. [NPC] George: Your Taming level cap has been increased!|
|Giving EPIC ([[Rarity]]) or better [[Giraffe Pet]]|[NPC] George: I've always wanted a Epic Giraffe? [NPC] George: I'm curious to see if they really are as tall as the legends say. [NPC] George: Your Taming level cap has been increased!|
|Giving EPIC ([[Rarity]]) or better [[Jellyfish Pet]]|[NPC] George: Do you think the Epic Jellyfish can dance? [NPC] George: I've always been mesmerized by their underwater ballet. [NPC] George: Your Taming level cap has been increased!|
|Giving EPIC ([[Rarity]]) or better [[Bal Pet]]|[NPC] George: Thank you for getting the Epic Bal. [NPC] George: It's a bit too hot for me down there! [NPC] George: Your Taming level cap has been increased!|
|Giving EPIC ([[Rarity]]) or better [[Baby Yeti Pet]]|[NPC] George: I'm glad you could fetch me a Epic Baby Yeti? [NPC] George: I cannot stand the cold! [NPC] George: Your Taming level cap has been increased!|
|Giving RARE ([[Rarity]]) or better Frost Wisp Pet ([[Wisp Pet]])|[NPC] George: Now, I am be delighted to see a Rare Frost Wisp. [NPC] George: Their icy presence is said to be quite refreshing. [NPC] George: Your Taming level cap has been increased!|
|Maxing out Taming Level Cap|[NPC] George: You've done it! [NPC] George: You've brought me all the pets I've asked for, and in doing so, have shown exceptional dedication and skill. [NPC] George: Your Taming level cap can now reach the maximum level of 60, thanks to your efforts. [NPC] George: You've not only helped me complete my collection but also demonstrated the true spirit of a master tamer. [NPC] George: Remember, the world of taming is vast and ever-changing. [NPC] George: May your adventures continue to be as fruitful and exciting as this quest has been!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] George: ✆ Oh absolutely you can have my contact info. [NPC] George: ✆ It may look like I'm always here at my desk, but really I'm always traveling. [NPC] George: ✆ Lately I've been looking across the universe for 3 specific pets. [NPC] George: ✆ The [pet], [pet] and [pet] pets. [NPC] George: ✆ If you can bring me ALL of those, you can have my contact! [NPC] George: ✆ Oh, by the way, they should all be Lvl 1. [GIVE PETS]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving [[Pets]]|[NPC] George: ✆ That's fantastic! [NPC] George: ✆ I love pets! [NPC] George: ✆ Here take my contact. [NPC] George: ✆ You'll be able to reach me while I am or you are in the wilderness.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|

## Trivia
- George generally offers an extremely low amount of money compared to their value for most pets, with some exceptions such as milestone pets.
- You can donate a higher rarity pet than requested for [[Taming]] level cap increases, however George's dialogue will still say it is the rarity they asked for rather than the actual rarity.
- There is a limit of 50,000,000 [[Coins]] that can be earned from selling pets to George per day.

# Igrupan
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters
[Igrupan]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Mage Faction Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) Help Dean ([[Quests]])
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Tasks the player with finding his 5 Chickens ([[Igrupan's Chickens]]) during the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]), as well as killing [[Chickzilla]] later on.
Location: [[Igrupan's Chicken Coop]]
Coordinates: -31 93 -824
Igrupan is an [[NPC]] who owns a chicken coop in [[Scarleton]].
## Chickzilla
Chickzilla spawns after the player talks to Igrupan and completes his quest. The player must have more than [[Factions|7,000 ቾ Mage Reputation]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before Quest|[NPC] Igrupan: I'm busy working on my coop.|
|Beginning of Quest; (After helping [[Dean]])|[NPC] Igrupan: Every. Single. One. [NPC] Igrupan: I wake up this morning and all my chickens are GONE. [NPC] Igrupan: I finally found animals immune to Sulphur mutations but breeding them is a misery. [NPC] Igrupan: Could you help me getting them back inside?|
|Looking for Chickens Random|[NPC] Igrupan: I quit my previous job for this. It HAS to work.|
|Looking for Chickens Random|[NPC] Igrupan: My chickens can't be that far, I have 5 of them.|
|Bringing a Chicken Back Random|[NPC] Igrupan: Oh [chicken] was missing too? Wondered why it was so peaceful.|
|Bringing a Chicken Back Random|[NPC] Igrupan: [chicken] Jr., what time to do you call this?|
|Bringing a Chicken Back Random|[NPC] Igrupan: Oh [chicken], I'm so glad to have you back!|
|Bringing a Chicken Back Random|[NPC] Igrupan: Finally! [chicken] has been returned to me!|
|Bringing a Chicken Back Random|[NPC] Igrupan: Oh there they are! Nice to see you again Regi... I mean [chicken].|
|Returned All Chickens|[NPC] Igrupan: I don't know how to thank you! [NPC] Igrupan: You just saved my business! [NPC] Igrupan: Come back in a few days and I'll reward you with my first chicken!|
|[[Chickzilla]] has Spawned|[NPC] Igrupan: A SUPER CHICKEN! [NPC] Igrupan: I was wrong!! My chickens are not immune to Sulphur! [NPC] Igrupan: One of my chickens mutated into an abomination. [NPC] Igrupan: I don't know what to do, it's way to strong for me!|
|After [[Chickzilla]] is Killed|[NPC] Igrupan: I can't believe you managed to slay this creature of nightmares. [NPC] Igrupan: I wish I could reward you but I am completely ruined. [NPC] Igrupan: I am never going to financially recover from this...|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) [[Chickzilla]] can be Spawned|[NPC] Igrupan: ✆ HELP!!!!! [NPC] Igrupan: ✆ MY CHICKENS... THEY MUTATED! [NPC] Igrupan: ✆ COME TO THE CHICKEN COOP QUICKLY!!!!!|
|After Finishing Quest|[NPC] Igrupan: I'll be more careful from now on! [NPC] Igrupan: If you have an Abiphone you should add me to your contacts!|
|Below 7,000 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Igrupan: Maybe come back when you are more experienced for more. [NPC] Igrupan: Build yourself some reputation first, then we can talk again. [NPC] Igrupan: Come back later and I will have more for you to see.|
|While [[Chickzilla]] is Alive|[NPC] Igrupan: What am I going to do? My business is finished! [NPC] Igrupan: Are you strong enough to defeat it?|
|After [[Chickzilla]] is Killed|[NPC] Igrupan: Maybe I should try to get my old job back... [NPC] Igrupan: I will never forget Chickzilla.|
|Not in the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]); (Random)|[NPC] Igrupan: Sorry, I only work with Mages...|
|Not in the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]); (Random)|[NPC] Igrupan: I don't recognise you stranger, clear off.|
|Not in the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]); (Random)|[NPC] Igrupan: Guards! Stranger danger! Keep them away from my chickens!|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Mage 1/4)|[NPC] Igrupan: Oh, a new face! Welcome to the City of Magic! [NPC] Igrupan: We used to be Pig Farmers centuries ago, but thanks to Sulphur we are now superior beings capable of mastering the magical elements! [NPC] Igrupan: If you are interested in tourist attractions I suggest you visit our Cathedral, which is also our Academy now.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); Less than 1,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Igrupan: [amount] Intelligence! That's...ok I guess!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); 1,000-2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Igrupan: [amount] Intelligence! Wow that's not bad at all!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); More than 2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Igrupan: [amount] Intelligence! What, how? Who are you even?|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 3/4)|[NPC] Igrupan: Have you tried the new ability phone? [NPC] Igrupan: The new one is barely better than the one from last year, people still buy them like sheep... [NPC] Igrupan: Wait, you don't even have an Abiphone? [NPC] Igrupan: Yikes...|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 4/4)|[NPC] Igrupan: Ah, the Piglins yes, quite troublesome they are. [NPC] Igrupan: I wouldn't go there if I were you.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Igrupan: You seem suspicious.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Igrupan: Sorry, I can't talk to you right now.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Igrupan: Please stop bothering me.|
## Trivia
- The chicken by the name of Chickovsky is likely a reference to a Russian composer Pyotr Ilyich Tchaikovsky.
- The chicken, Chikira, is a possible reference to a Colombian singer Shakira.
- Igrupan's Minigame is possible a reference to the cucco minigames in The Legend Of Zelda series.
- One of Igrupan's responses to being below [[Factions|7,000 ቾ Mage Reputation]] is likely referencing an often satirized quote from controversial community member Larucus:
"get a reputation before you talk, cause in my eyes, you ain't got one. Maintain your position for a long time in relevancy, then we'll talk again"
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Oct 19, 2022|0.16|Added Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]).|

# Jacob
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Jacob]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Hosts Jacob's Farming Contests ([[Jacob's Farming Contest]]).
Location: [[Farmhouse]]
Coordinates: 46.5 73 -128.5
Jacob is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Farmhouse]] and [[The Garden]]. He hosts [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Farming Contests]].
## Event
Jacob's Farming Contest is an [[Events|event]] that takes place every 3 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Days]], and lasts for 1 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Day]] during which three random crops from the [[Collections|Farming Collection]] are selected for players to collect.

I coordinate a farming contest every few SkyBlock days, eh? [NPC] Jacob: Check ye calendar eh? Or better yet, click me to learn more, eh!|

## Trivia
- Jacob is the first NPC shown to be directly affected by an active [[Mayor Election|Mayor]].
- Bulk Claiming [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Contest]] rewards at Jacob has a limit of only being able to claim the rewards from 28 (one full page) contests at once.
  - If you have more than 28 contest rewards to claim, Jacob will say:
[NPC] Jacob: Hot diggity, that's a lot of medals! I gave ya some rewards, speak with me if you're interested in the rest.
- If a contest is close enough, the 2nd message from surpassing your [[Anita|Personal Best]] may interrupt the dialogue from the end of the event, in which case the dialogue will finish prematurely.
- He is a brother-in-law to [[Finnegan]], which implies at least one of them is married.

# Kaus
Aliases: Barbarian Training, Barbarian training, Duelist Kaus
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Kaus]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Duels the player 7 different times to prepare against fighting [[Rollim]].
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -596 113 -640
Kaus is an [[NPC]] found in [[Dragontail]]. He requests for the player to fight him daily to train him for a duel against [[Rollim]].
## Quest
Upon defeating him, he can be fought again after 24 hours, getting progressively stronger until the final fight on the 7th day. After defeating him on the 7th day, he gives the player a [[Bag of Gold]] as a reward for training him. The [[Bag of Gold]] sells for 250,000 [[Coins]] to NPCs.
### Training
Kaus will increase their [[Health]] each day, and deal increasing damage.
Kaus will run towards the player and deal damage to them using a [[Wooden Sword]]. If the player is too far away, they will fire a few shots from their [[Bows|Bow]]. After 3/4 of their [[Health]] has been depleted, they will eat a [[Golden Apple]] to regenerate back up to 3/4 of their original [[Health]].
|Day|Health|Damage|Reward for defeating|
|-|-|-|-|
|1|50,000 [[Health]]|200 [[Damage]]|25 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]])|

# Maxwell
Aliases: Stats Tuning, Thaumaturgist (NPC), Thaumaturgy, Tuning, Tuning Points
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Maxwell]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to change Accessory Powers ([[Powers]]) on their [[Accessories]].
Location: [[Thaumaturgist]]
Coordinates: -66.5 70 -66.5
Maxwell is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Thaumaturgist]]. He allows players to change the [[Powers|Accessory Powers]] on their [[Accessories]].
## Usage
### Unlocking More Powers
More advanced powers can be unlocked by giving Maxwell 9x [[Power Stones]] of the same type.
### Stats Tuning
Maxwell allows the player to tune the stats they want, so that they can optimize their setup to their liking. The stats that can be tuned are:
- [[Health]]
- [[Defense]]
- [[Speed]]
- [[Strength]]
- [[Crit Damage]]
- [[Crit Chance]]
- [[Attack Speed]]
- [[Intelligence]]
Every 10 [[Accessory Power]] grants 1 Tuning Point. Tuning Points are used to tune a specific stat to increase it. The player can remove Tuning Points from stats if they want to, in case they want to invest them in another stat. The player is offered tuning templates, in which the player can save and load their stats. First template is free, the others cost 10,000 [[Bits]] to unlock each.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction First interaction|[NPC] Maxwell: Accessories are X magical X pieces of gear. [NPC] Maxwell: To truly harness their power, collect as many as possible and store them in your Accessory Bag! Select an option: [Ok, then what?]|
|First Interaction Selecting [Ok, then what?]|[NPC] Maxwell: On top of their existing abilities, each accessory makes your Accessory Bag more powerful! [NPC] Maxwell: Accessories add some ACCESSORY POWER to the bag depending on their rarity. Select an option: [Accessory Power?]|
|First Interaction Selecting [Accessory Power?]|[NPC] Maxwell: Yes! The more Accessory Power, the more stats like ❤ Health or ✎ Intelligence you get from your Accessory Bag. Select an option: [That's amazing!]|
|First Interaction Selecting [That's amazing!]|[NPC] Maxwell: No, it's magic! [NPC] Maxwell: Even better, YOU choose what stats you get! [NPC] Maxwell: Check it out!|
|Combining 9x Identical [[Power Stones]]|[NPC] Maxwell: [Player], that's an excellent power!|
|Combining 9x Identical [[Power Stones]]|[NPC] Maxwell: I'm not so sure about this power, it's used by some dark wizards.|
|Combining 9x Identical [[Power Stones]]|[NPC] Maxwell: A wise choice, [Player]!|
|Combining 9x Identical [[Power Stones]]|[NPC] Maxwell: I've learned this power myself, [Player].|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Maxwell: ✆ You want my Abiphone contact? [NPC] Maxwell: ✆ That way you'll be able to access my features from anywhere? [NPC] Maxwell: ✆ Isn't that cheating though? [NPC] Maxwell: ✆ Don't you know you are supposed to walk all the way here every single time? [NPC] Maxwell: ✆ Ah well, times are changing I suppose... [NPC] Maxwell: ✆ Plus it's a magical object, so it's not that bad. [NPC] Maxwell: ✆ Now let's see, what should I ask for it... [NPC] Maxwell: ✆ What about some more Luxurious Spool, hum? [NPC] Maxwell: ✆ I'm joking! Bring me Ultimate Carrot Candy x2. [NPC] Maxwell: ✆ I have pets to feed. [GIVE ITEMS]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving 2 [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]]|[NPC] Maxwell: ✆ My frogs are going to love that, thanks a lot!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Without 2 [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]]|[NPC] Maxwell: ✆ You don't have what I need! Nice try!|

# Pablo
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Pablo]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Asks for flowers in exchange for 25 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]]) every 2 days.
Location: [[Scarleton]]
Coordinates: -130 127.5 -815.5
Pablo is an [[NPC]] located in [[Scarleton]], on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Quest
Pablo will ask the player for a certain Flower to make Dyes. Once the player gives the specific Flower to Pablo, the player will gain [[Factions|25 ቾ Mage Reputation]].
Flowers:
- [[Enchanted Dandelion]]: 45%
- [[Enchanted Poppy]]: 45%
- [[End Stone Rose]]: 10%
The quest can be completed once every 2 days.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Pablo: Hello. [NPC] Pablo: I am a modest painter. [NPC] Pablo: Painting is a way to escape the harsh reality; I can create my own flawless world. [NPC] Pablo: I paint all sorts of things, but it costs a lot of colored dyes. [NPC] Pablo: Could you help me and bring me some flowers? Most flowers cannot spawn on the Crimson Isle. [NPC] Pablo: I need a [item].|
|Without Item|[NPC] Pablo: Could you bring me a [item]?|
|Incorrect Item|[NPC] Pablo: That is not the correct flower I'm looking for. [NPC] Pablo: Bring me that [item] as soon as you can! [NPC] Pablo: These paintings don't paint themselves!|
|Giving Correct Item; First Interaction|[NPC] Pablo: Thank you for supporting my passion. [NPC] Pablo: I often need flowers, feel free to come back in a few days.|
|Interacting With Correct Item|[NPC] Pablo: Great, back to painting!|
|After Giving Correct Item|[NPC] Pablo: I can sense a great deal of virtue in you. You are a source of inspiration.|
|In Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Pablo: I need quiet, please.|
|In Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Pablo: Sorry, but I need to concentrate.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Pablo: ✆ Hello, [player]! [NPC] Pablo: ✆ Are you available? I desperately need a <item> today. [NPC] Pablo: ✆ Please meet me at my house if you have one. I would greatly appreciate it!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Without Item|[NPC] Pablo: ✆ Bring me that [flower] as soon as you can! [NPC] Pablo: ✆ These paintings don't paint themselves!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; After Giving Correct Item|[NPC] Pablo: ✆ I don't need any flowers at the moment!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; As a ⚒ Barbarian ([[Factions]])|✆ No answer.|
|Obtaining [[The Soup]] Given any soup|[NPC] Pablo: Thank you so much. [NPC] Pablo: Here, I want you to have another painting. You claimed a The Soup!|
## Trivia
- The name Pablo is a reference to the famous painter Pablo Picasso.

# Professor Wynd
Aliases: Wynd
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Professor Wynd]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Explains the [[Weather]] mechanic.
Location: [[Weather Station]]
Coordinates: 2.5 180 60.5
Professor Wynd is an [[NPC]] in the [[Weather Station]] at the top of the [[Mountain]]. They explain the [[Weather]] mechanic.
## Usage
With Professor Wynd's [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]], the player can have up to 4 weather alerts active at a time, each tied to a specific weather type in a specific island.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Professor Wynd: Have you noticed any peculiar weather recently? [NPC] Professor Wynd: That's the Origin Cloud's doing - it seems to amplify even the faintest of weather patterns, turning them into something extreme. [NPC] Professor Wynd: Sunny and I have soared far and wide in my flying contraption in pursuit of documenting the Origin Cloud! [NPC] Professor Wynd: However, all it took was one rogue lightning bolt to send us crashing into this mountain. [NPC] Professor Wynd: We're both fine, but it's going to take a while to fix up our ship, given that it's... well, embedded in the mountain. [NPC] Professor Wynd: While we're busy dealing with that, feel free to stop by anytime. [NPC] Professor Wynd: I'll happily show you everything I know about the weather of each SkyBlock island with you. [NPC] Professor Wynd: Here, why don't you take a look?|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Professor Wynd: ✆ Ah, the critically acclaimed Abiphone. The wonders of technology! [NPC] Professor Wynd: ✆ I wouldn't mind notifying you about the weather every now and then, but there's one issue... [NPC] Professor Wynd: ✆ You see... I used to have this contraption that allowed me to control the weather... [NPC] Professor Wynd: ✆ It resembled a Wand, but I doubt you'd go through the trouble of acquiring such a marvel of science. [GIVE ITEM]|

[NPC] Professor Wynd: ✆ I can finally wield the power to control the weather once more! [NPC] Professor Wynd: ✆ Let's give it a test shall we? [NPC] Professor Wynd: ✆ That's it? They don't make them like they used to. [NPC] Professor Wynd: ✆ Oh well, a deal's a deal. [NPC] Professor Wynd: ✆ Since we're buddy-buddy now, I'll grant you access to my Weather Alert System. [NPC] Professor Wynd: ✆ Once you've set it up, you'll automatically get a call from me when my systems detect your desired weather in your desired location! ## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|

|First Interaction|[NPC] Queen Nyx: What does a stranger have to do with the Taika people? Speak! [YOU] I have something to give you: [CLICK TO SHOW]|
|Showing [[Letter of Recommendation]]|[NPC] Queen Nyx: A letter of recommendation from the Dean, let me read this. [NPC] Queen Nyx: ... [NPC] Queen Nyx: Intriguing to say the least. [NPC] Queen Nyx: I have the upmost faith in our Dean, if they value your company then I'll welcome you to our faction. [NPC] Queen Nyx: But if you are up to no good, I'll revoke this access immediately. [NPC] Queen Nyx: Now go, and enjoy our great city, new Faction member!|
|During the [[Mage Council]] meeting|[NPC] Dean: Oh, <player> is here! They're the one who retrieved the research! [NPC] Queen Nyx: I see, we owe you adventurer, this object is of great value to us. [NPC] Dean: Yes indeed! With it we are able to extract salt from Netherrack, it will be a massive asset when cooking. [NPC] Dean: You see, while we evolved past the need for crops and cattle, the quality of our food remains low. [NPC] Dean: I would say close to 50% of our research is still spent on food artifacts and machines. [NPC] Captain Ahone: And once again those Piglins stole our research. [NPC] Captain Ahone: They believe we are working on war engines. [NPC] Captain Ahone: The tension is rising and soon we might have to cross swords with them. [NPC] Queen Nyx: Captain Ahone, this isn't a conversation we should have right now. [NPC] Queen Nyx: Thank you very much, <player>. Would you mind leaving us now, we have matters to discuss. [NPC] Dean: <player> please meet me back at the Academy, we have a lot to talk about!|
|In the [[Mage Council]], after the meeting|[NPC] Queen Nyx: You are a little bit too close to me, keep your distances.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Queen Nyx: ✆ My contact? [NPC] Queen Nyx: ✆ Maybe if you reach enough reputation...but maybe not...|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Below 12,000 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) With 12,000 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Queen Nyx: ✆ You have quite the reputation around here! [NPC] Queen Nyx: ✆ I suppose you have more than earned my contact! Here you go!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Queen Nyx: ✆ I didn't give you my contact for this reason. [NPC] Queen Nyx: ✆ Only call me if you have something urgent to talk about.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Queen Nyx: Us Taika people love the taste of Melon Juice so much that we'd go just about anywhere to get some.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Queen Nyx: Normally I'd send somebody else to do my bidding for me, but this is an urgent matter.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Queen Nyx: Do you have the faintest idea who you're talking to? I won't forget this!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[NPC] Queen Nyx: The Mage Faction is growing, we will soon uncover all the secrets of Mana.|
|None|[NPC] Queen Nyx: I can sense great power in you.|
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Trying to insert an item that isn't the [[Letter of Recommendation]] in the Present Item menu|They are not interested in this item!|
## Trivia
- Queen Nyx is a reference to the goddess Nyx from Greek mythology. She is the personification of night.
- It is possible to give Queen Nyx a [[Bachelor's Rose]] while in the [[Factions|⚒️ Barbarian Faction]] if the player has met the 12,000 [[Factions|ቾ Mage Reputation]] requirement. This can be done by etherwarping through the floor and quickly giving the Bachelor's Rose to Queen Nyx before the guards teleport the player out.

# Roddy
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Roddy]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: One Man's Trash ([[Quests]])
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to apply [[Rod Parts]] to [[Fishing Rods]].
Location: [[Backwater Bayou]]
Coordinates: 23.5 78 -24.5
Roddy is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Backwater Bayou]] that can apply [[Rod Parts]] to [[Fishing Rods]].
## Usage
### Applying Rod Parts
To apply [[Rod Parts]] to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], the player can click on a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] in their inventory and then the [[Rod Parts|Rod Part]] they want to apply.
### Removing Rod Parts
The [[Coins]] cost for removing a [[Rod Parts|Rod Part]] scales with the Rod Part's rarity:
|Rarity|Cost|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10,000 [[Coins]]|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|25,000 [[Coins]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|50,000 [[Coins]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|100,000 [[Coins]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|250,000 [[Coins]]|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|500,000 [[Coins]]|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|
### Converting Old Rods
Due to 0.22 Backwater Bayou, several [[Fishing Rods]] were changed into [[Rod Parts]]. Attempting to fish with them displays the message:
This rod is broken and cannot be used to fish!
|Icon|Name|Rarity|Replacement Rod Part|Replacement Fishing Rod|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Prismarine Rod]]|[[Prismarine Rod]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|[[Prismarine Sinker]]|[[Challenging Rod]]|
|[[Sponge Rod]]|[[Sponge Rod]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|[[Sponge Sinker]]|[[Challenging Rod]]|
|[[Speedster Rod]]|[[Speedster Rod]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|[[Speedy Line]]|[[Challenging Rod]]|
|[[Farmer's Rod]]|[[Farmer's Rod]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])||[[Challenging Rod]]|
|[[Ice Rod]]|[[Ice Rod]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|[[Icy Sinker]]|[[Challenging Rod]]|
|[[Winter Rod]]|[[Winter Rod]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|[[Festive Sinker]]|[[Challenging Rod]]|
|[[Yeti Rod]]|[[Yeti Rod]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Icy Sinker]]|[[Challenging Rod]]|
|[[Shredder]]|[[Shredder]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|[[Shredded Line]]|[[Challenging Rod]]|
|[[Phantom Rod]]|[[Phantom Rod]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|[[Spooky Hook]]|[[Challenging Rod]]|
|[[Auger Rod]]|[[Auger Rod]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|[[Icy Sinker]]|[[Rod of the Sea]]|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before talking to [[Junker Joel]]|[NPC] Roddy: Junker Joel wants to talk to you about Junk! [NPC] Roddy: I can help you apply Rod Parts after!|
|Introduction|[NPC] Roddy: How's it going, champ? [NPC] Roddy: If you ever want to apply Rod Parts to your fishing rods, I'm your guy! [NPC] Roddy: Hooks make you more likely to catch certain things. [NPC] Roddy: Lines grant bonus stats, no matter where you are! [NPC] Roddy: Sinkers also grant bonuses, but only in certain areas! [NPC] Roddy: If you ever want to apply a Rod Part or have any questions about them, come see me!|
|After applying a Junk Sinker in the tutorial|[NPC] Roddy: There ya go, your rod is now equipped with a Junk Sinker! [NPC] Roddy: I can apply Hooks, Lines, and Sinkers to your fishing rods at any time! [NPC] Roddy: You can also remove them, but that'll cost you some Coins!|
|Clicking with an Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) without their contact|[NPC] Roddy: Hey pal! You lookin' for the contact, huh? [NPC] Roddy: Alright, pop a squat and listen up then, buddy. [NPC] Roddy: This morning I got woken up by a Banshee screechin' outside my window. [NPC] Roddy: Before I knew it, she snatched something from my washin' line and leapt back into the Bayou. [NPC] Roddy: If you get that piece of Torn Cloth back off the Banshee, I'll give you my contact.|
|Without a Torn Clotch|[NPC] Roddy: Where's my Torn Cloth, champ?|
|With a Torn Cloth|[NPC] Roddy: You got my Torn Cloth back! [NPC] Roddy: Well, what's left of it... [NPC] Roddy: Not gonna be able to wear this anymore, bud. But a deal's a deal, I guess! [NPC] Roddy: Here's my contact, boss!|

# Rollim
Aliases: Duelist Rollim
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Rollim]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Duels the player 7 different times to prepare against fighting [[Rollim]] Rollim.
Location: [[Scarleton]]
Coordinates: -147 106 -854
Rollim is an [[NPC]] found in [[Scarleton]]. He requests for the player to fight him daily to train him for a duel against [[Kaus]].
## Quest
Upon defeating him, he can be fought again after 24 hours, getting progressively stronger until the final fight on the 7th day. After defeating him on the 7th day, he gives the player a [[Bag of Gold]] as a reward for training him. The [[Bag of Gold]] sells for 250,000 [[Coins]] to NPCs.
### Training
Rollim will increase their [[Health]] each day, and deal increasing damage.
Rollim will run towards the player and deal damage to them using a [[Wooden Sword]]. If the player is too far away, they will fire a few shots from their [[Bows|Bow]]. After 3/4 of their [[Health]] has been depleted, they will eat a [[Golden Apple]] to regenerate back up to 3/4 of their original [[Health]].
|Day|Health|Damage|Reward for defeating|
|-|-|-|-|
|1|50,000 [[Health]]|200 [[Damage]]|25 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|
|2|100,000 [[Health]]|300 [[Damage]]|25 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|
|3|250,000 [[Health]]|400 [[Damage]]|25 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|
|4|1,000,000 [[Health]]|500 [[Damage]]|25 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|
|5|2,500,000 [[Health]]|750 [[Damage]]|25 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|
|6|10,000,000 [[Health]]|1,000 [[Damage]]|25 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|
|7|25,000,000 [[Health]]|2,500 [[Damage]]|25 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]]); [[Bag of Gold]]|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|

[NPC] Rollim: The problem is that I'm not a fighter, at ALL! [NPC] Rollim: Would you mind helping me train a little bit? You look like a seasoned veteran, no offense. Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|Introduction Day 2|[NPC] Rollim: I'm ready to be defeated again... Are you ready? Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|Introduction Day 3|[NPC] Rollim: I barely recovered from the last training session but I'm ready, are you? Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|Introduction Day 4|[NPC] Rollim: I can feel that I'm slowly growing stronger, are you ready for today's training session? Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|Introduction Day 5|[NPC] Rollim: Only 3 days left to train, are you ready? Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|Introduction Day 6|[NPC] Rollim: I feel so strong today, are you ready? Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|Introduction Day 7|[NPC] Rollim: It has already been a week, time flies! [NPC] Rollim: Are you ready for my last training session? Select an option: [YES] [NO]|

|Selecting [YES]|[NPC] Rollim: 3 [NPC] Rollim: 2 [NPC] Rollim: 1 [NPC] Rollim: En garde!|
|Selecting [NO]|[NPC] Rollim: Welp, no hard feelings, I guess.|

|After fight Day 2|[NPC] Rollim: That really hurts, but I need to get stronger at ALL COST! [NPC] Rollim: Please come back tomorrow.|
|After fight Day 3|[NPC] Rollim: Wow! [NPC] Rollim: You are truly on another level... [NPC] Rollim: Please come back tomorrow.|
|After fight Day 4|[NPC] Rollim: You still beat me effortlessly... [NPC] Rollim: I really hope I'll be ready on time. [NPC] Rollim: Please come back tomorrow.|

|After fight Day 6|[NPC] Rollim: I really don't deserve such a skilled trainer. [NPC] Rollim: Please come back tomorrow.|
|After fight Day 7|[NPC] Rollim: I've grown so much over the last week thanks to you. [NPC] Rollim: Just a week ago I was stressed and scared, now I feel confident. [NPC] Rollim: I am finally ready for my duel. [NPC] Rollim: Please accept this, I can't thank you enough. [NPC] Rollim: I'll prepare for my duel now. [NPC] Rollim: I think I'll become a motivational speaker. [NPC] Rollim: Crazy to think I could improve so much in a week. [NPC] Rollim: I want to improve every day, for the rest of my life.|
|After Day 7|[NPC] Rollim: Hello! [NPC] Rollim: I went to the duel yesterday but we both agreed not to fight. [NPC] Rollim: I feel so much more sure of myself! I've grown beyond my old futile conflicts. [NPC] Rollim: This was a life changing experience.|

# Sirih
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Sirih]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Gives the player 10 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]]) for turning them back into a Pigling, and talks about stories they are writing.
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -699.5 131 -887.5
Sirih is an [[NPC]] located in [[Dragontail]].
## Quest
Once Sirih turns into a [[Pig]], the player can give them 1 [[Sulphur]]; this can only be done if the player is a Barbarian. After doing so, the player will be rewarded with [[Factions|10 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]].
Sirih will return to their [[Pig]] form after 24 hours.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Interacting while Sirih is a [[Pig]]|[NPC] Sirih: Oink.|
|Giving [[Sulphur]] for the first time|[NPC] Sirih: What, when? Oh. [NPC] Sirih: How long have I been gone? [NPC] Sirih: I keep on forgetting about Sulphur... [NPC] Sirih: Nowadays I live as a recluse, focusing on my work. [NPC] Sirih: I'm a writer, so I need calm to concentrate on my new novel. [NPC] Sirih: I really need to get back to work.|
|First Interaction; In Barbarian Faction|[NPC] Sirih: The name of my new novel? Hmm... [NPC] Sirih: I haven't decided yet. [NPC] Sirih: I mostly write fictions. Because you helped me, I'll tell you some of the plot. [NPC] Sirih: The story takes place in a dystopian Crimson Isle where the lava of the Volcano is turning into water. [NPC] Sirih: We Piglins always dream of water. This book will be about showing that water also brings pain and struggle.|
|Giving [[Sulphur]]|[NPC] Sirih: Again... [NPC] Sirih: I get so absorbed by my work... [NPC] Sirih: Time really flies when you work on something you love. [NPC] Sirih: Thanks for the Sulphur! I'll finish my new novel now.|
|Interacting while Sirih is NOT a [[Pig]]|[NPC] Sirih: My new novel? [NPC] Sirih: It's about an old hero that everyone still considers as the strongest. [NPC] Sirih: But he himself knows very well that his prime is over and that his next fight will be his last. [NPC] Sirih: It's be a sad story about how the expectations of others can force you to do things that hurt you.|
|Interacting while Sirih is NOT a [[Pig]]|[NPC] Sirih: My new novel is about someone who has a strange power that allows him to inspect the memories of others. [NPC] Sirih: The first part shows him having fun with his powers and exploring ways to use memories to his advantage. [NPC] Sirih: But one day he finds a strange character, and in their memories he sees things he should have never seen... [NPC] Sirih: That's it! I won't tell you more than that!|
|Interacting while Sirih is NOT a [[Pig]]|[NPC] Sirih: I'm currently working on a complex love story. [NPC] Sirih: A smart and beautiful girl is forced to marry someone they have never met before. [NPC] Sirih: She's apprehensive at first but when she meets him they end up surpassing all expectations. [NPC] Sirih: She falls in love quickly but he doesn't and for the first time in her life she has to deal with that emotional pain. [NPC] Sirih: It's a simple story but it touches a lot of interesting subjects.|
|Interacting while Sirih is NOT a [[Pig]]|[NPC] Sirih: I'm writing a fictional story about Kaeman and his wife Sarah, before the Calamity. [NPC] Sirih: Most people know Kaeman as the Wither King, but I think it's important to remind people that he once was a human. [NPC] Sirih: The tragedy of his life isn't very known. He had an idyllic relationship with his wife, up until their daughter was born. [NPC] Sirih: I'm trying to show how pain and sorrow turned this well respected man into an evil doer.|
|Interacting while Sirih is NOT a [[Pig]]|[NPC] Sirih: My new book is a different take on the battle royale stories. [NPC] Sirih: Every 10 years there is a global tournament to the death with all the strongest warriors in the world. [NPC] Sirih: The winner of the tournament becomes immortal. [NPC] Sirih: Every 250 years there is a special battle royale tournament with the previous 24 winners. [NPC] Sirih: The twist is that those battle veterans are accustomed to their immortality, which has impacted their views and personalities over time. [NPC] Sirih: It's a bit cheesy, but you gotta make money sometimes.|
|Interacting while Sirih is NOT a [[Pig]]|[NPC] Sirih: I think this new story will be my masterpiece. [NPC] Sirih: But this time the inspector he framed escaped the justice system and hunts him down. [NPC] Sirih: It's an incredible battle of wits between two brilliant minds.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Trying to obtain the Contact ([[Abiphones]]); while Sirih is a [[Pig]]|[✆] Oink.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling while Sirih is a [[Pig]]|[NPC] Sirih: Oink?|

|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling while Sirih is NOT a [[Pig]]|[NPC] Sirih: ✆ Is this urgent? I'm working on my new novel at the moment.|

# Sirius
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Dark Auction
[Sirius]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Allows the player to sign up for the [[Dark Auction]].
Location: [[Wilderness]]
Coordinates: 91.5 75 176.5
Description: Hosts the [[Dark Auction]].
Location: [[Dark Auction]]
Coordinates: 86 55 153
Sirius is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wilderness]]. He hosts the [[Dark Auction]].
## Overview
Sirius hosts the [[Dark Auction]]. Every 3 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Days]] (1 real-life hour) at approximately the 55th minute of every hour, Sirius will stand outside the Dark Auction and allow players with over 400,000 [[Coins]] to enter. Players who do not have sufficient coins will not be allowed to enter the lobby, and only the richest players will be able to enter.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[Wilderness]] First Interaction|[NPC] Sirius: Are you here for the Auction? Only the richest players can enter.|
|[[Wilderness]] Above 400,000 [[Coins]]|[NPC] Sirius: You signed up for the Auction! [NPC] Sirius: Wait a bit while other players join...|
|[[Wilderness]] Below 400,000 [[Coins]]|[NPC] Sirius: You can't even afford to bid! Come back when you have at least 400,000 coins!|
|[[Wilderness]] While in the queue|[NPC] Sirius: You are signed up for the Auction!|
|[[Wilderness]] Not enough coins to join Dark Auction|[NPC] Sirius: You didn't have enough coins to make it into the Auction! [NPC] Sirius: The poorest player allowed in had # Coins!|
|[[Wilderness]] If a player signs up with greater than 400,000 [[Coins]], but enters with 0 [[Coins]].|[NPC] Sirius: You don't have enough coins? Get out!|
|[[Wilderness]] If there are less than 5 players in a Dark Auction lobby|[NPC] Sirius: Sorry folks we are going to have to cancel the show for tonight, please come back in 3 days for the next auction!|
|[[Wilderness]] On Ⓑ Bingo ([[Bingo]])|[NPC] Sirius: You don't need to enter the Dark Auction to complete the Bingo.|
|[[Wilderness]] Talking during Great Spook ([[The Great Spook]]) with 7,871,265 [[Coins]] in [[Purse]]|[NPC] Sirius: Oh. [NPC] Sirius: That's the exact amount of coins I got from selling the first gift my father gave me. [NPC] Sirius: It's now my favorite number. [NPC] Sirius: I wrote a book about my childhood, do you want it? Select an option: [Yes of course]|
|[[Wilderness]] Selecting [Yes of course]|[NPC] Sirius: Here, take it. [NPC] Sirius: I hope you will like it. You claimed a Shadows indicate riddles, illuminating unseen stars!|
|[[Dark Auction]]||
|[[Dark Auction]] None; In [[Dark Auction]]|[NPC] Sirius: Shh, keep it down!|
|[[Dark Auction]] Introduction|[NPC] Sirius: Come on down to the basement, the auction is about to begin! [NPC] Sirius: Welcome to the Dark Auction! I hope everyone's ready, the first item's about to start! [NPC] Sirius: First up we have a <item>, the starting bid is # Coins! [NPC] Sirius: The bidding will start in #s. [NPC] Sirius: You can place your bids now!|
|[[Dark Auction]] Bids|[NPC] Sirius: <player> placed a bid of # Coins!|
|[[Dark Auction]] If you are the highest bidder on an item and have Sirius's Abiphone contact unlocked|[NPC] Sirius: ✆ Pstt. Don't tell anyone, but I've refunded you <amount> Coins for being such a loyal customer.|
|[[Dark Auction]] Bidding Ends|[NPC] Sirius: The highest bidder was <player> with a bid of # Coins! [NPC] Sirius: Next up we have a <item>, the starting bid is # Coins!|
|[[Dark Auction]] If no one places any bids|[NPC] Sirius: No one made any bids! Let's move on to the next item!|
|[[Dark Auction]] Ends|[NPC] Sirius: Alright show's over folks! Come back in 3 nights for the next auction.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Attempting to add Abiphone contact without [[Sirius' Personal Phone Number]]|[NPC] Sirius: ✆ Let me guess, you're yet another player who desires my contact... [NPC] Sirius: ✆ Well, I'm afraid you'll have to get in line with everyone else. [NPC] Sirius: ✆ If you come to enough auctions, you'll find what you're looking for. [NPC] Sirius: ✆ Though you'll still have to outbid everyone else, of course!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|✆ No answer. He's a busy man, after all.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Picking up the pre-Dark Auction call|[NPC] Sirius: ✆ Hello. This is an automated message being sent to all of my closest contacts. [NPC] Sirius: ✆ The next Dark Auction will be taking place very soon. Same time, same place as usual. [NPC] Sirius: ✆ If you are interested in making a purchase, please begin to make your way over.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Sirius: I've always wanted to start a farm but instead I got sucked into this life of crim...I mean business!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Sirius: This smell... it reminds me of my childhood when I used to pick flowers in the garden. Keep this between you and me, understand?|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Sirius: I'll be back. I know you'll make the right decision.|
## Trivia
- Based on his dialogue both as a [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]] and when giving the player [[Shadows indicate riddles, illuminating unseen stars|Shadows indicate riddles illuminating unseen stars]], he is quite sentimental of his childhood.
## Bugs
- It is common for Sirius to call players who have his [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]] and remind them about the Dark Auction significantly later than intended. It is possible for the player to receive the call reminding them of the dark auction after Sirius has despawned.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Added Sirius.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Improved Sirius' dialogue to better explain the [[Dark Auction]].|

# St. Jerry
Aliases: St Jerry
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Events, Season of Jerry, Quests, Quest giver
[St. Jerry]
Type: [[NPC]]

St. Jerry is an [[NPC]] located in [[Jerry's Workshop]]. If the player finds all 20 [[White Gift|White Gifts]] scattered around [[Jerry's Workshop]], they will be rewarded with a [[Green Gift]].

# Suus
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Suus]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Asks the player with finding the [[Toy]] for [[Turd]] in exchange for +40 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]]) every 2 days.
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -615.5 115 -706.5
Suus is an [[NPC]] located in [[Dragontail]].
## Quest
If the player is a member of the Barbarian [[Factions|Faction]], Suus asks the player to find [[Turd]]'s [[Toy]] by killing [[Magma Cube Rider|Magma Cube Riders]]. If the player contacts via [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] call, Suus will give a different mob.
The Toy has a 10% chance to drop. Returning the Toy will reward the player with [[Factions|+40 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]]. This can be done once every 2 days.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction; In Barbarian Faction|[NPC] Suus: My kid has been crying for hours. [NPC] Suus: We just came back from our walk and a monster ate his favorite toy. [NPC] Suus: One of the Magma Cube Riders out there did it!|
|After Finding [[Toy]]|[NPC] Suus: You found his toy! [NPC] Suus: Thank you so much [player]!|
|Giving Suus [[Toy]]; First Time|[NPC] Suus: My little Turd has such a lovely voice, I don't mind hearing him cry. [NPC] Suus: Such an expensive toy, I am glad you found it for him!|
|Giving Suus [[Toy]]; Except First Time|[NPC] Suus: Thanks for finding his toy again, [player]! [NPC] Suus: He keeps losing it...|
|Abiphone Contacts ([[Abiphones]])|[NPC] Suus: ✆ The monsters ate his toy again! I don't know what to do! [NPC] Suus: ✆ This time it was a [Mob]! [NPC] Suus: ✆ Can you come back here and help?|
|Not in Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]); (Random)|[NPC] Suus: Get away from my child!|
|Not in Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]); (Random)|[NPC] Suus: Step away from my child, stranger!|
|Not in Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]); (Random)|[NPC] Suus: Don't talk to strangers, little Turd.|
|Not in Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]); (Random)|[NPC] Suus: Stop harassing me and my child!|
|Not Found [[Toy]]; (Random)|[NPC] Suus: I don't think he will ever calm down until he gets his toy back.|
|Not Found [[Toy]]; (Random)|[NPC] Suus: This kid is a handful.|
|Not Found [[Toy]]; (Random)|[NPC] Suus: Maybe one of the Magma Cube Riders out there will have his toy.|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 1/4)|[NPC] Suus: Are you a Wizard? [NPC] Suus: Well you don't look like one, and you don't smell like one either. [NPC] Suus: In that case, I have nothing against you. I wish you a sulphuric day sir.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 2/4)|[NPC] Suus: Hello stranger. Are you looking to join our faction? [NPC] Suus: What a fantastic choice! [NPC] Suus: We have a range of motives far deeper and more interesting than those terrible fire mages. [NPC] Suus: Joining our faction is quite hard but I'm sure an incredible individual like you will manage!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 3/4)|[NPC] Suus: Sul-phur [NPC] Suus: Sulphur? [NPC] Suus: Me Sulphur.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 4/4)|[NPC] Suus: Are you a Hero? [NPC] Suus: Did you come to free us from the everlasting threat of the wizards? [NPC] Suus: The mighty Volcano is protecting us, but for how long?|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Suus: You seem suspicious.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Suus: Sorry, I can't talk to you right now.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Suus: Please stop bothering me.|
## Trivia
- Suus's name could be a reference to the term "sus," short for "suspicious," popularized by the video game Among Us.
  - The name could also be a reference to the scientific name of a pig, Sus scrofa domesticus, since the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] used to be pigs.

[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 8, 2022|Added Suus.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Suus.|
