# Trevor
Aliases: Trapper, Trapper Animal, Trapper Animals, Trevor the Trapper
Categories: Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Outdated pages, Quest giver
[Trevor]
Type: [[NPC]]
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to hunt down animals in exchange for [[Pelts]].
Location: [[Trapper's Den]]
Coordinates: 281.5 104 -542.5
Trevor is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Trapper's Den]]. He gives out quests to the player which involve tracking down and hunting an animal in exchange for [[Pelts]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Trevor: You will have 10 minutes to find the mob from when you accept the task. [NPC] Trevor: Any longer than that and the animal will run away! Accept the trapper's task to hunt the animal? Click an option: [YES] - [NO]|
|Selecting [NO]|[NPC] Trevor: If you change your mind, you know where to find me!|
|Selecting [YES]|[NPC] Trevor: You can find your [animal rarity] animal near the [location].|
|On Cooldown|[NPC] Trevor: Sorry I don't have any animals for you to hunt. [NPC] Trevor: Try coming back in [#]s!|
|During a Task|[NPC] Trevor: You already have a mob spawned! Hunt that mob first!|
|Error|[NPC] Trevor: I couldn't locate any animals. Come back in a little bit!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Trevor: ✆ Eh, I still have an Abiflip. [NPC] Trevor: ✆ It has 3 contact slots and I already have Tammy and Tony. [NPC] Trevor: ✆ I heard the new models are nice, but they're too smart for me! [NPC] Trevor: ✆ I could add you for let's say... 24 Pelts and 1,000,000 coins? [PAY PELTS AND COINS]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Trevor: ✆ That's awesome, but now my Abiflip is full.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Not Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Trevor: ✆ You don't have what it takes!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; In [[Mushroom Desert]]|[NPC] Trevor: You will have 10 minutes to find the mob from when you accept the task. [NPC] Trevor: Any longer than that and the animal will run away! Accept the trappers task to hunt the animal? Click an option: [YES] - [NO]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Not In [[Mushroom Desert]]|[NPC] Trevor: ✆ I'm currently hunting! Don't call again!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Trevor: I've been tracking an animal for the past few days. Got anything I could use as bait?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Trevor: This should do nicely. I'll have plenty of pelts by the end of the day.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Trevor: Looks like I'll have to catch it the old fashioned way.|
## Tasks
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated.
You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
Reason: Tracking and Finnegan Changes
This article was last edited by Adamimoka (contribs|logs|block) on 1:13, September 17 2026 (40 days ago)
Upon interacting with Trevor, he will offer the choice to start a quest to find and kill an animal. Accepting the task will spawn a mob will be somewhere on the [[Mushroom Desert]].
The mob is invisible to other players, but is not immune to external damage. If the mob manages to die from environmental damage, the [[Pelts]] will still be rewarded to the player, as if the player had killed it themselves. The mob's behavior is decided based on its rarity and type. [[Tracking]] increases the chance to find Elusive animals.
Given the chances below, players will earn an average of ≈4.12 [[Pelts]] per quest. This is increased to ≈4.15 [[Pelts]] per with [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL IX (9)]], as well as an additional 1 [[Pelts|Pelt]] each with [[Pelt Belt]], another 1 [[Pelts|Pelt]] each with [[Jake's Plushie]], totaling to ≈9.15 [[Pelts]] per quest with all buffs.
Other buffs are added before the multiplier from [[Finnegan]] (discontinued), and the amount of [[Pelts]] rewarded is rounded up.
|Rarity|Ability|Stats|Rewards|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Trackable|Nothing; These animals don't really do much to defend themselves!|100 [[Health]]|2 [[Pelts]]; 50 [[Hunting]] XP; 1 Coin ([[Coins]])|39.6039%; (240 weight)|
|Untrackable|Mobility; These animals run slowly away from you when you approach them!|500 [[Health]]|4 [[Pelts]]; 100 [[Hunting]] XP; 10 [[Coins]]|29.7029%; (180 weight)|
|Undetected|Quickness; These animals run quickly away from you when you approach them!|1000 [[Health]]|6 [[Pelts]]; 250 [[Hunting]] XP; 100 [[Coins]]|19.8019%; (120 weight)|
|Endangered|Blind; Blinds you when you get close.; Quickness; These animals run very quickly away from you when you approach them!|5000 [[Health]]|8 [[Pelts]]; 500 [[Hunting]] XP; 1,000 [[Coins]]|9.9009%; (60 weight)|
|Elusive|Blind; Blinds you when you get close.; Quickness; These animals sprint away from you when you approach them.; Awareness; Disappears if it sees you.|10000 [[Health]]|16 [[Pelts]]; 3,000 [[Hunting]] XP; 10,000 [[Coins]]|0.9900%; (6 weight) Enderman Slayer IX (9) ([[Enderman Slayer]]):; ~1.1532%; (7 weight)|
Animal: Ability
Cow: Cowmouflage; Goes invisible when the hunter gets near.
Pig: Pig Stench; Emits a stench that makes the hunter nauseous.
Sheep: Ramming; Sends the player flying when they get near.
Rabbit: Bouncy; Jumps extra high.
Chicken: Nothing; Just flails around.
Horse: Teleport; Runs really, really fast.
## Trivia
- They are [[Finnegan]]'s cousin, meaning they share an unknown grandparent.
